  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Rising, engulfing force supplies a hydraulic mechanism in which human capacity becomes pressure beyond measure.

## familiarity disarms limits
- reading: Overstepping may arise precisely when familiarity and comfort make the human feel that no boundary remains dangerous.
- mechanism: Familiarity removes estrangement and can also remove caution. The human oversteps from inside a sphere made safe and ordinary, so transgression incubates in comfort rather than only in hostility.
- trace:
  - 96:6 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B003: Comfort that removes estrangement supplies the low-friction interior in which vigilance toward limits can lapse.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B001: Rebellious boundary-crossing supplies the consequential movement that familiarity alone cannot explain.

## received measure decalibrated
- reading: The human de-calibrates from a measure already built into creation and made readable through teaching.
- mechanism: Gathering, measuring, distinguishing, and trimming form a calibration sequence around creation and teaching. The focus verb then reads as a deliberate departure from a measure already assembled, marked, and made legible.
- trace:
  - 96:6 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B001: Visible human presence identifies the recipient of formation and instruction who later acts in public.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B001: Crossing a limit supplies the breach whose prior calibration the context now exposes.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B001: Gathering and assembling supply the first operation by which dispersed material becomes an ordered readable whole.
  - 96:1 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring and proportioning supply an originating standard against which later excess can register.
  - 96:4 **عَلَّمَ** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing mark that guides makes the limit perceptible rather than hidden.
  - 96:4 **بِٱلْقَلَمِ** ق ل م B001: Cutting and trimming hard material to level it supplies a concrete calibration technology for the teaching sequence.

## dependency claims independence
- reading: The human exceeds limits by treating a nurtured, attached existence as if it were self-originating and sufficient.
- mechanism: Attachment and nurturing completion produce a dependent human, but self-perceived sufficiency recodes that received formation as autonomous possession. Tughyan becomes enacted denial of the relations that made the agent possible.
- trace:
  - 96:6 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B001: The visible human is the formed dependent who later presents as an autonomous public agent.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B001: Boundary-crossing is recast as the behavior through which dependence is practically denied.
  - 96:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing, repairing, and bringing to completion supply an ongoing source of the human's capacity.
  - 96:2 **عَلَقٍ** ع ل ق B001: Clinging attachment supplies the human's originating condition of dependence and relation.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Claimed independence supplies the cognitive recoding that converts received capacity into imagined self-sufficiency.

## bestowed growth counterfeit height
- reading: Tughyan is a counterfeit height: received growth hardens into a self-grounded position that forgets its giver.
- mechanism: The context offers several forms of elevation as naming, nurtured increase, and bestowed honor. The focus root's raised-rock image then distinguishes legitimate uplift from a hardened height that presents itself as self-grounded.
- trace:
  - 96:6 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B001: Visible human presence locates the contrast in a person whose status can be displayed before others.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B005: A smooth raised outcrop supplies the image of elevated, hardened self-positioning.
  - 96:1 **بِٱسْمِ** س م و B001: Elevation and rising supply a contextually prior upward motion associated with the invoked name.
  - 96:3 **وَرَبُّكَ** ر ب ب B001: The non-dominant mapped image of increase and elevation makes growth something received under lordship.
  - 96:3 **ٱلْأَكْرَمُ** ك ر م B001: Bestowed honor and praiseworthy generosity distinguish gifted elevation from self-exalting height.

## closed self gaze
- reading: The human transgresses when a reflected self becomes the whole field of evidence and is mistaken for proof of independence.
- mechanism: The human sees a self-image, treats that image as proof of sufficiency, and closes the field of vision around the self. The later assertion of divine seeing reopens that field, so tughyan becomes a perceptual feedback loop rather than abundance alone.
- trace:
  - 96:6 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B005: The small human image in the pupil supplies a literal self-image around which a closed gaze can form.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Overflow supplies the result when the reflected self expands beyond its proper scale.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B006: View, spectacle, and mirror imagery turn self-seeing into an enclosed reflective apparatus.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Imagined independence is the conclusion the subject draws from the self-confirming image.
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B001: Disclosure to a knower makes the forgotten fact of a wider field cognitively available.
  - 96:14 **ٱللَّهَ** ء ل ه B001: The worshipped divine referent supplies the observer outside the human's self-enclosed frame.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Actual seeing by eye or insight reopens the field and reverses the subject's assumption that only self-perception counts.

## return contains overflow
- reading: Tughyan is an outbound surge inside a larger return circuit; it exceeds a local bank but never exits the sustaining relation.
- mechanism: The one-way surge of the focus is placed inside a larger return circuit. Return to the nurturing source, reinforced by returning-water imagery, makes tughyan an outbound excursion that cannot abolish the encompassing relation.
- trace:
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: A rising, engulfing force supplies the apparent one-way escape from containment.
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing completion identifies the destination of return as the same sustaining relation the surge seemed to leave.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B001: Return to a prior state closes the outbound vector into a larger circuit.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B006: Returning water materially folds the focus flood image back toward its source.

## monopoly over limits
- reading: The transgressive human claims the authority to set another's limit while refusing the corresponding endpoint for himself.
- mechanism: The transgressor stops another person's worship yet refuses to arrive at his own stopping point. Tughyan therefore becomes asymmetrical boundary administration: assigning an end to another while exempting oneself from an end.
- trace:
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B001: Crossing the proper bound supplies the asymmetry between limits imposed outward and limits rejected inward.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B004: The split-root image of an overbearing tyrant makes private excess operational as coercive rule over another.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Forbidding and restraining supply the outwardly imposed stop.
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Humble worship identifies the other person's yielded orientation that coercive authority tries to interrupt.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B003: The specified act of worship makes the imposed boundary bodily and observable.
  - 96:15 **يَنتَهِ** ن ه ي B002: Reaching an endpoint supplies the withheld self-limit demanded of the one who had limited another.

## vertical reversal at the head
- reading: Tughyan is embodied as hardened self-height, reversed either by seizure at the forelock or by voluntary lowering that paradoxically brings nearness.
- mechanism: A raised, hardened human position is reversed at the body's front: the forelock can be seized from above, while refusal of false compliance permits voluntary bowing and nearness. Vertical height and relational proximity move in opposite directions.
- trace:
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B005: The raised outcrop supplies a material profile for the human's hardened self-elevation.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Seizing by the forelock supplies the involuntary reversal of the elevated posture.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The forelock localizes the reversal at the leading, self-presenting part of the body.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B001: Obedience supplies the allegiance that must first be refused before bodily orientation can change.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B004: Bowing the head supplies a voluntary downward motion that answers the earlier self-elevation.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Nearness makes voluntary lowering relationally ascending even while it is spatially downward.

## voice scaled into counterforce
- reading: Human tughyan can become a noisy social technology: a claim voiced into an assembly, scaled into coercive pressure, and answered by counter-mobilization.
- mechanism: A human noise becomes a summons, the summons gathers an assembly, and speech acquires the force of a shove. The answering summons calls a repelling force, so tughyan scales socially and then meets a structurally matched counter-call.
- trace:
  - 96:6 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B001: Visible human presence supplies the individual voice that can recruit a public collective.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B008: The split-root image of a people's voice or noise supplies an acoustic form of excess that can scale beyond one person.
  - 96:17 **فَلْيَدْعُ** د ع و B001: Calling and drawing by speech convert private noise into an outward summons.
  - 96:17 **فَلْيَدْعُ** د ع و B001: The non-dominant mapped image of a forceful shove makes the summons an attempt to move others, not mere sound.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B003: The assembled council supplies the social body through which one person's excess multiplies.
  - 96:18 **سَنَدْعُ** د ع و B001: The answering call mirrors the transgressor's summons and reverses who controls mobilization.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: Pushing, collision, and prevention supply the counterforce that answers socially amplified pressure.

## redirected obedience and nearness
- reading: Tughyan is misdirected allegiance organized around a false center; its reversal requires refusing that obedience and reorienting the body toward nearness.
- mechanism: The focus excess is finally exposed as a rival orientation system rather than quantity alone. Refusing its claim breaks false authority; humble bodily orientation then produces nearness in the opposite direction.
- trace:
  - 96:6 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B004: The human-facing side supplies an orientation anchor: the issue is what the person turns toward and lets face him.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B003: False transgressive authority supplies the rival center that demands compliance and diverts from good.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B001: Obedience identifies the relational channel through which the false center would reproduce its excess.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B001: Humble lowering supplies the contrary bodily orientation after false obedience is refused.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Drawing near supplies the relational result of redirected obedience and lowering.

## saturation spills over
- reading: Perceived sufficiency fills the self past capacity, so tughyan is the spillover of imagined saturation.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:6 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B002: Perceiving something into awareness supplies the self-diagnosis of being full or sufficient.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Surging beyond bounds supplies the spill that follows imagined saturation.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B008: The non-dominant image of fullness that thickens and straightens supplies the material state the self believes it has reached.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the perceived completion that leaves no room for further dependence.

## deceptive self surface
- reading: Tughyan can operate as a self-image that misreports dependence, then turns away to keep that deceptive surface intact.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:6 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B005: The miniature human image in the eye supplies a self-representation that can be mistaken for the whole person.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B001: Boundary-crossing makes the misleading image an enacted distortion rather than harmless appearance.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B006: Mirror and spectacle imagery supplies the reflective surface on which the self-representation is trusted.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B009: A garment that lies by its appearance supplies the model of an outward surface misreporting the condition beneath it.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Turning away supplies the maneuver that protects the deceptive self-surface from corrective inspection.

## extended neck column
- reading: Tughyan appears as extended-neck, column-like self-elevation whose rigid vertical claim is undone by bowing.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:6 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B004: The person-facing side supplies a bodily orientation from which neck, head, and posture can be tracked.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B005: The smooth raised outcrop supplies the focus image of rigid self-elevation.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B002: The non-dominant long-neck image turns elevation into an extended bodily posture.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B003: The upright column image intensifies the posture as rigid vertical self-presentation.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B004: Bowing the head supplies the explicit counter-motion that breaks the neck-column geometry.


# Focus 96:7

## self certifying independence
- reading: His own perception certifies, for him, that he no longer needs an outside source; the verse foregrounds self-certification rather than proving actual independence.
- mechanism: Self-observation becomes self-warrant: the subject treats his reading of his present condition as proof that no outside support is needed.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye or insight supplies the self-observation that initiates the certification loop.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Self-sufficiency and reduced need supply the state that the observer attributes to himself.

## independence as verdict
- reading: He judges himself able to stand in for what he formerly needed; independence is a cognitive verdict with substitutive force.
- mechanism: The subject issues a verdict about his own adequacy and then lets that verdict substitute for every relation on which he might otherwise rely.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B002: Opinion and reflective judgment make the seeing an evaluative conclusion rather than a neutral visual report.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing and standing in for another turn adequacy into a claim that the self can replace needed supports.

## public self image
- reading: He internalizes a visible performance of sufficiency, so the supposedly private self-image is partly manufactured by an imagined audience.
- mechanism: A public or mirrored image feeds back into identity: an appearance of abundance is staged, observed, and accepted by the subject as evidence of real independence.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B005: Showing an act before people supplies the social display through which sufficiency becomes performative.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B006: Visible appearance and the mirror supply the reflected image that the subject can mistake for his substance.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Wealth and reduced need provide the status communicated by the displayed image.

## settlement mistaken for autonomy
- reading: He mistakes being settled for being source-independent, projecting a stable interval into permanent autonomy.
- mechanism: Duration and settled possession look permanent from inside a stable moment; that visible stability is converted into a claim of independence.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B002: Reflective judgment supplies the inference from a stable present to a supposedly permanent condition.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B004: Settled dwelling and long residence supply the durable-looking condition mistaken for autonomy.

## saturation threshold
- reading: He experiences himself as quenched or full and mistakes the pause in need for the abolition of need.
- mechanism: A bodily or material threshold of fullness is read as a metaphysical threshold: because the subject feels filled, he concludes that further supply and every supplier are unnecessary.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B001: Quenching and freedom from thirst supply the felt threshold at which demand temporarily falls silent.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B008: Fullness that thickens and settles turns temporary saturation into an apparently stable self-state.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Reduced need converts the sensation of fullness into the conclusion that dependence has ended.

## received knowledge appropriated
- reading: He mistakes read, taught, and inscribed capacity for self-produced competence, so epistemic reception is erased at the moment it becomes usable.
- mechanism: Reading, disclosure through teaching, and the shaped writing instrument make competence visibly mediated; the focus subject then appropriates those received capacities as evidence that he is self-originating and needs no source.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B002: Reflective judgment supplies the self-assessment that converts acquired competence into an autonomy claim.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Reduced need supplies the conclusion falsely drawn from possession of learned capacity.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B002: Reading, recitation, and causing another to read supply a transmissive act rather than self-generated knowledge.
  - 96:4 **عَلَّمَ** ع ل م B001: Disclosure to a knower supplies the event by which what was unavailable becomes known.
  - 96:4 **بِٱلْقَلَمِ** ق ل م B003: The pared writing reed supplies the external material mediation that preserves and transfers knowledge.

## status mark misread as essence
- reading: He reads a name, reputation, or visible status-mark as proof of inner independence, confusing social legibility with ontology.
- mechanism: A name or visible mark makes a status legible; the focus subject sees the legible sign of abundance and treats designation or reputation as the underlying reality of independence.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B006: Visible appearance and mirror-image supply the surface that can be mistaken for the whole self.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Wealth and reduced need supply the status attributed to the visible surface.
  - 96:1 **بِٱسْمِ** س م و B005: A name as announcement and indication supplies the designation through which status becomes socially readable.
  - 96:1 **بِٱسْمِ** س م و B001: A visible mark that makes a thing known supplies the outward sign the subject treats as essence.

## gift effect misread as source
- reading: He uses the success of nurture and generosity to erase the nurturer and giver; the completed gift becomes evidence against the relation that produced it.
- mechanism: Nurture, completion, and generous giving produce the subject's sufficiency; once the gift is present, he reads its effect as evidence that the giver is dispensable.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B002: Reflective judgment supplies the attribution by which an effect is reassigned to the self.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Possession and reduced need supply the successful effect that conceals its provider.
  - 96:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture, repair, and completion supply the ongoing process that brings the subject to adequacy.
  - 96:3 **ٱلْأَكْرَمُ** ك ر م B001: Honored generosity supplies the gift whose possession can be misread as self-originated wealth.

## created attachment denied
- reading: He narrates himself as uncaused and unattached, turning temporary functional adequacy into denial of constitutive dependence.
- mechanism: Creation brings the subject into existence through attachment; the focus judgment of independence therefore becomes a denial not merely of current help but of constitutive, origin-level dependence.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B002: Reflective judgment supplies the present-tense verdict that overwrites the subject's dependent origin.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Independence and reduced need supply the claim placed in tension with attachment.
  - 96:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B002: Bringing creation into existence supplies the subject's received rather than self-caused beginning.
  - 96:2 **عَلَقٍ** ع ل ق B001: Clinging to and remaining attached to another supplies dependence as the material condition of emergence.

## saturation becomes overflow
- reading: The mistaken conclusion abolishes his sense of boundary, so received fullness changes phase into force that exceeds its proper limit.
- mechanism: The split-root fullness latent in focus word 2 meets the immediately preceding image of water exceeding its boundary: saturation becomes dangerous when the subject interprets being full as being independent and therefore accepts no limiting bank.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B001: Quenching supplies the momentary cessation of thirst that is misread as permanent freedom from supply.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B008: Filled-out settled fullness supplies the accumulated condition poised to cross a boundary.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Reduced need supplies the mistaken interpretation placed on saturation.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Water rising with sweeping force supplies the boundary-crossing consequence of uncontained fullness.

## return voids substitution claim
- reading: His autonomy is a local snapshot inside a return circuit whose destination retains ownership; he cannot replace the source to which he returns.
- mechanism: The focus subject judges himself able to suffice or stand in for what he needs, but the immediate return to the sustaining owner makes that substitution temporary and revocable.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B002: Reflective judgment supplies the subject's claim that present adequacy settles the matter.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing and replacing supply the strongest version of the autonomy claim challenged by return.
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Ownership and sustaining lordship supply the destination whose claim the self cannot replace.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B001: Return and restoration to a prior point supply the reversal of imagined self-standing.

## counter gaze breaks self certification
- reading: His self-view is only one testimony under counter-observation; the verse exposes an epistemic conflict over who gets to certify the self.
- mechanism: The private act of seeing oneself is surrounded downstream by repeated demands that another observer consider the scene and by a final seeing attributed beyond the subject; self-certification becomes one contested viewpoint inside a field of counter-seeing.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye or insight supplies the private viewpoint on which the independence claim rests.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Self-sufficiency supplies the conclusion whose validity depends on which gaze governs.
  - 96:9 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The alerting tell-me formula recruits a second evaluator and opens the private verdict to public examination.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye or insight supplies the encompassing counter-gaze that observes the self-observer.

## self sufficiency as gatekeeping
- reading: It is a political error that turns self-certification into permission to police another person's relation of worship.
- mechanism: The inward verdict of adequacy acquires institutional force: one who treats himself as sufficient begins to restrain another's dependent worship, acting as though his judgment can replace the other's governing relation.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B002: Reflective judgment supplies the private verdict that is projected outward as authority.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B002: Standing in for another supplies the implied usurpation by which the self appoints itself as sufficient authority.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Restraint and prevention supply the coercive operation through which the autonomy claim governs another body.
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Submissive worship supplies the relation whose enactment the gatekeeper tries to block.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B003: The specific act of worship supplies the concrete practice targeted by prohibition.

## denial maintains closed reading
- reading: He actively stabilizes that view by falsifying what challenges it and withdrawing from the gaze that could correct it.
- mechanism: The self-sufficiency verdict is maintained by an active feedback process: counterevidence is declared false, then the subject turns away so no further evidence can revise the judgment.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B002: Opinion and judgment supply the belief that must be protected from revision.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B004: Settled dwelling supplies an image of a judgment made habitual and resistant to displacement.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B001: Contradiction of truth supplies the rejection of evidence that would reopen dependence.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Turning the back and withdrawing supply the exit that prevents corrective encounter.

## borrowed club strength
- reading: He mistakes access to a callable network for power located inside himself; the challenge makes the hidden social dependency audible.
- mechanism: The supposedly independent subject is tested by being told to summon his assembly; his resort to a social body reveals that his confidence is infrastructural, while the counter-summons exposes force as relational rather than self-contained.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B001: Self-seeing supplies the mistaken viewpoint from which borrowed backing appears to be an intrinsic property.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Independence and means supply the claim falsified by the need to summon allies.
  - 96:17 **فَلْيَدْعُ** د ع و B001: Calling and drawing others by speech supply the act that exposes reliance on external responders.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B001: The gathered council supplies the social body from which the subject borrows apparent power.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: Pushing, collision, and prevention supply the counter-force that reveals power as an opposed relation.

## lowering opens nearness
- reading: Refusal of the autonomy claimant, followed by embodied dependence, creates proximity; the focus posture is not freedom but distance.
- mechanism: The final sequence reverses the focus geometry: refusing compliance with the self-sufficient claimant and bodily lowering oneself do not produce deprivation or distance; lowering becomes the motion by which proximity is gained.
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B002: Self-judgment supplies the false geometry in which autonomy appears elevated and dependence appears low.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Independence and reduced need supply the posture reversed by chosen submission.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B001: Compliance and obedience supply the allegiance explicitly refused to the self-sufficient gatekeeper.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B001: Bodily lowering and humility supply the downward movement that overturns the autonomy posture.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Nearness as the opposite of distance supplies the result generated by lowering rather than self-elevation.

## hydraulic dependency circuit
- reading: The self is a basin inside a circulation: inflow creates fullness, denial of inflow produces overflow, and return plus renewed approach reveal dependence as cyclical.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B001: Quenching supplies the focus basin's temporary experience of having no thirst.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Reduced need supplies the basin's mistaken claim that inflow is no longer constitutive.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Sweeping high water supplies overflow when temporary fullness accepts no bank.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B006: Returning rain or water supplies recirculation that disproves possession as an isolated endpoint.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B003: Dew and rain moisture supply dispersed replenishment beyond the supposedly self-contained basin.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B008: Approach undertaken in search of water supplies renewed movement toward a source.

## echoing voice as self warrant
- reading: He voices and circulates an account of his adequacy, then mistakes its echo and social amplification for independent confirmation.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B003: Relaying reports and poetry supplies a narrated self that can circulate as testimony about its own adequacy.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B003: Song and vocal modulation supply the performed voice whose force can be mistaken for independent authority.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B002: Recitation supplies the initial voiced transmission from which later self-performance is derived.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B007: Repeated sound and echo supply the returning voice that can be misheard as external confirmation.
  - 96:17 **فَلْيَدْعُ** د ع و B001: Calling by raised speech supplies the attempt to turn voice into mobilizing power.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B001: Raised voice and proclamation supply the public acoustic field in which the self-narrative is amplified.

## reflected human image
- reading: The self sees an image that can exist only on a reflective surface, in another pupil, or under another gaze, so even the image of independence is relationally produced.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B006: Mirror and visible appearance supply the reflected self-image on which the autonomy judgment rests.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Wealth and reduced need supply the identity the reflected image is made to display.
  - 96:1 **بِٱسْمِ** س م و B002: A visible sign by which an observer reads a condition supplies the social coding of the reflected image.
  - 96:6 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B005: The human image in the pupil supplies the literal dependence of a seen self-image on an eye that receives it.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: A second act of seeing supplies the external gaze within which the supposedly autonomous image remains held.


# Focus 96:8

## sovereign reversion
- reading: Every temporary claim of possession or independence is destined to revert to the Lord's already-existing authority.
- mechanism: The return branch moves what has gone out back to a prior holder or state, while the lordship branch identifies that endpoint as owner and authority. Return is therefore a reversion of custody and jurisdiction, not merely travel toward a place.
- trace:
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Ownership and authority make the Lord the rightful holder to whom all apparent autonomy reverts.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B001: Return or sending back supplies the movement of restoration to that holder.

## formative completion
- reading: Return is also the creature's re-entry into the care and completion from which its development never became independent.
- mechanism: The destination is not only sovereign but the one who has been bringing the addressed creature stage by stage toward completion. The return can therefore complete or restore a formative relation rather than simply transfer a subject to a tribunal.
- trace:
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture, repair, and completion make the Lord the continuing agent of formation.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B014: Recovery of a former or better condition lets return function as restoration under that care.

## answer returned
- reading: A person's deeds and claims come back as an answer that must be rendered to the Lord.
- mechanism: A returned answer converts motion back to the Lord into answerability: what issued into action or speech comes back as a response before authority.
- trace:
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Lordship supplies the authoritative addressee and evaluator of the returned response.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B005: A reply returned to its addressee turns return into responsive accounting.

## abiding recurrence
- reading: The final return remains live, but it crowns repeated feedbacks to an abiding Lord already occurring within the line.
- mechanism: The Lord remains while rain, sound, and other motions recur. This permits a cyclical model in which return is not exhausted by one terminal transfer: repeated movements continually feed back toward an abiding source.
- trace:
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B007: Staying, clinging, and duration supply the stable pole around which returns recur.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B006: Recurrent water supplies a material cycle of going out and coming back.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B007: Returning sound supplies a second, auditory form of recurrence around the stable endpoint.

## inscribed readback
- reading: The answer is the creature's read, taught, marked, and enacted life returned as a legible readback to its authoring Lord.
- mechanism: Recitation, an identifying name or mark, measured creation, Lord-formed knowledge, and the writing instrument form an authorship-to-inscription chain. The reply branch of `الرجعى` closes that chain as readback: formed and taught life returns legible to its source.
- trace:
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B005: A returned reply gives the focus return a channel of readback rather than bare relocation.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B002: Reading and recitation supply voiced or legible material capable of being returned.
  - 96:1 **بِٱسْمِ** س م و B001: The split mapping's visible identifying mark lets the name participate as inscription.
  - 96:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B003: Lord-formed knowledge identifies the named Lord as the chain's teaching source.
  - 96:1 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Measured formation supplies an authored design rather than an unstructured beginning.
  - 96:4 **عَلَّمَ** ع ل م B002: An identifying sign makes taught knowledge readable as evidence.
  - 96:4 **بِٱلْقَلَمِ** ق ل م B003: The sharpened writing instrument materializes the inscription and readback mechanism.

## dependence restored
- reading: Return specifically reverses the fiction of self-sufficiency and makes original dependence on the nurturer visible again.
- mechanism: The human begins in attachment and under nurture, then judges itself self-sufficient. Return restores the relation that the independence claim concealed: the dependent is brought back into manifest dependence on the sustaining Lord.
- trace:
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and completion anchor the destination as the sustainer of an unfinished dependent.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B014: Restoration of condition makes return the recovery of a denied creaturely relation.
  - 96:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: The same nurturing title at the opening links the creature's beginning and endpoint.
  - 96:2 **عَلَقٍ** ع ل ق B001: Clinging and dependence supply the human's originating relational condition.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Claimed self-sufficiency attempts to sever the relation that return restores.

## overflow recontained
- reading: Return is also the countercurrent of transgression: self-swollen overflow is receded and recollected into the source it could not exceed.
- mechanism: Self-sufficiency produces boundary-crossing like rising, sweeping water. The focus roots also carry collected and recurrent water, allowing return to appear as the counter-movement in which overflow is made to recede into a containing source.
- trace:
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B013: Abundant collected water supplies a contained reservoir at the Lordward endpoint.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B006: Returning water supplies the receding or cycling motion back toward containment.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Rising, sweeping water turns transgression into a material overflow beyond bounds.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: The sufficiency claim supplies the pressure that the reader links to overflow.

## gaze returned as evidence
- reading: The person's very way of seeing and self-acquitting returns as evidence beneath the Lord's counter-gaze.
- mechanism: The human first issues a self-verdict of sufficiency; repeated demands to see interrupt that verdict; lying then stands under disclosed divine sight. Return as reply becomes evidentiary: the self-authored view comes back for correction under the Lord's seeing.
- trace:
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Authority gives the returned evidence a competent evaluator.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B005: The returned answer lets perception and claim come back as testimony.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B002: The heart's opinion supplies the self-assessing judgment that will be reviewed.
  - 96:9 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The first alerting `have you seen` reopens what self-perception treated as settled.
  - 96:11 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The repeated inquiry keeps the conduct under examination.
  - 96:13 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The third inquiry forces the competing moral possibilities back into view.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B001: Contradiction of truth supplies the claim whose evidentiary return matters.
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B001: Disclosure to a knower makes the concealed conduct available for judgment.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Divine seeing replaces self-certification with an encompassing witness.

## prohibition rebounds
- reading: His attempt to stop another already carries its own return: the stopper is driven to his limit by the authority he tried to displace.
- mechanism: The same root first names one person's attempt to stop another and later demands that the stopper reach a stop. The return statement between them becomes a reversal operator: coercive arrest rebounds into the coercer's own terminus under higher authority.
- trace:
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Lordship establishes the superior jurisdiction that reverses the attempted prohibition.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B001: Sending back supplies the reversal that turns an imposed stop upon its agent.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Forbidding and checking action identify the coercion initially directed outward.
  - 96:15 **يَنتَهِ** ن ه ي B002: The endpoint at which a thing stops turns that coercive root back into the agent's own limit.

## return as course correction
- reading: Return is also a direction one can inhabit now by turning from the wrong course into worship, guidance, and protective alignment.
- mechanism: Worship, prayer, guidance, and protective orientation define a traversable route, while turning away defines its competing direction. The focus return can thus be enacted as turning back from a course and reorienting toward the nurturing Lord, without cancelling the final return.
- trace:
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and completion make the destination one toward which conduct can be re-formed.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B003: Turning back from an act supplies moral course correction inside the larger return.
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Humble worship supplies the subject's chosen orientation toward the true authority.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B003: The specified act of worship makes that orientation embodied and repeatable.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B001: Gentle direction toward a road or truth supplies a navigable Lordward course.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B002: Placing oneself within protection gives the course a preventive, self-reorienting function.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Turning away supplies the opposed vector that return can reverse.

## counter summons and custody
- reading: The local confrontation stages that reversion as a counter-summons that seizes the claimant and transfers him out of his council's protection.
- mechanism: The prohibitor summons his assembly; a counter-summons calls agents of seizure and repulsion; the body is taken by its forelock. Sovereign return becomes an executed transfer of custody, while returned reply becomes a call answered by a stronger call.
- trace:
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Ownership and authority make the physical retrieval an execution of superior custody.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B005: A returned answer lets the rival summons receive a concrete counter-answer.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Taking by the forelock converts return from abstraction into bodily retrieval.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The forelock as grasp point locates where custody is physically asserted.
  - 96:17 **فَلْيَدْعُ** د ع و B001: Calling by speech initiates the prohibitor's attempt to mobilize counter-authority.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B003: The gathered council supplies the social body on which the prohibitor relies.
  - 96:18 **سَنَدْعُ** د ع و B001: The answering call mirrors and overmatches the first summons.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: Pushing, striking, and preventing give the counter-summons enforcement force.

## return pre enacted as nearness
- reading: The worshipper begins that return willingly now: false obedience is refused, the body lowers, and Lordward nearness is entered before the final return.
- mechanism: Refusal of false obedience, bodily lowering, and approach form a present sequence of reorientation. The final command turns the future Lordward return into something voluntarily pre-enacted now, with the spatial paradox that lowering rather than self-elevation produces nearness.
- trace:
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B007: Abiding and drawing near make the Lordward endpoint available as present relation.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B003: Turning back from a prior course supplies the reorientation performed by the imperatives.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B001: Obedient yielding, here negated toward the prohibitor, clears the rival directional claim.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B001: Bodily lowering and humility enact chosen submission to the true authority.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Nearness supplies immediate arrival within the larger Lordward return.

## recitation as echoed return
- reading: The commanded recitation itself can sound the pattern of return: an utterance from Lord-formed knowledge repeats and answers back toward its source.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B007: Reverberating sound makes return audible as an echo or repeated recitation.
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B003: Lord-formed knowledge gives the repeated utterance a teaching source to which it can answer.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B002: The first recitation launches the sound whose recurrence can enact return.
  - 96:3 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B002: The repeated recitation gives the sonic-return analogy visible sequence support.

## returned matter remade
- reading: The return may include the creature's material being handed back for renewed forming by the same nurturing maker.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair and completion supply the agent who can re-form returned material.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B015: Reheated, reused, or reworked matter supplies a concrete model of return as remaking.
  - 96:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B002: Origination supplies the first transformation against which later re-formation can be imagined.
  - 96:2 **عَلَقٍ** ع ل ق B003: Thick clinging bodily matter gives the remaking analogy a context-supplied material substrate.

## vertical metric inverted
- reading: The final sequence inverts that metric: erect self-heightening is not approach, while lowering the head is the motion that becomes nearness.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: The non-dominant rise and increase image supplies a tempting upward metric for Lordward motion.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B001: Return supplies the reversal by which that apparent spatial metric can be corrected.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B002: The form-distant extended-neck image supplies an erect posture, retained only as a spatial analogy.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B004: Bending the head and bowing reverse the erect posture.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Nearness verifies that lowering, not self-heightening, is the successful approach.


# Focus 96:9

## interrogated restrainer
- reading: Attend closely and render judgment on the one whose defining act is making another stop.
- mechanism: The tell-me formula turns the clause from a sight report into a demand to inspect and judge a person identified entirely by the act of restraining someone else.
- trace:
  - 96:9 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The tell-me and alerting formula makes the opening a summons to examine a case rather than a yes-or-no question about eyesight.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Restraint from an act supplies the operation by which the otherwise unnamed person is identified.

## imposed endpoint
- reading: Can you recognize the one who appoints the point at which another person's course must end?
- mechanism: Seeing by insight combines with the endpoint branch of ن ه ي: the prohibitor does more than utter a negative command, attempting to decide where another person's course must terminate.
- trace:
  - 96:9 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye or insight asks the addressee to recognize the functional shape of the act.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B002: The endpoint reached by a thing renders prohibition as the imposition of a terminal boundary on another's trajectory.

## counterfeit reason
- reading: Use judgment on the apparent moral corrector: does his restraint express reason, or counterfeit it?
- mechanism: Reflective judgment is summoned to audit a would-be restrainer. The question leaves open whether his prohibition is genuine moral reason or merely a claim to reason's office.
- trace:
  - 96:9 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B002: Opinion and reflective judgment makes the interrogative an exercise of discernment.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B003: Reason named for restraining wrong supplies the moral office that the prohibiting actor appears to claim.

## transmission gate
- reading: The prohibitor becomes a gatekeeper trying to halt a chain in which insight is recited, taught, and made durable.
- mechanism: Repeated reading, disclosure through teaching, and the prepared writing instrument form a transmission chain. Against that chain, يَنْهَى can be heard as an attempted gate that stops knowledge from moving through voice, learner, and inscription.
- trace:
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Restraint from an act anchors the proposed interruption in the focus verb.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B002: Reading, recitation, and causing another to read supplies the vocal and transmissive activity exposed to interruption.
  - 96:4 **عَلَّمَ** ع ل م B001: Disclosure to a knower supplies the movement from hiddenness into learned recognition.
  - 96:4 **بِٱلْقَلَمِ** ق ل م B003: The trimmed writing stick supplies the durable material link in the chain that the restrainer would gate.

## development prematurely stopped
- reading: Have you seen one who cuts short a dependent act while it is still attaching, forming, and being brought toward completion?
- mechanism: Creation, attachment, and nurturing toward completion form a developmental mechanism. The endpoint branch of يَنْهَى then activates a counter-image: an agent who prematurely terminates what is still attaching, forming, and being brought to completion.
- trace:
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B002: The imposed endpoint anchors the model as a changed image of the focus prohibition.
  - 96:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing, repair, and completion supplies a process intended to reach fullness rather than be cut short.
  - 96:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B002: Creative bringing-into-being supplies the emergence whose continuation is at stake.
  - 96:2 **عَلَقٍ** ع ل ق B001: Attachment and persistent clinging supplies the fragile dependent phase that the imposed stop threatens.

## overflower as limit setter
- reading: Have you noticed the overflower who imagines himself sufficient and then appoints himself as the endpoint at which another must stop?
- mechanism: The human first exceeds a limit like rising floodwater, sees a sufficient image of himself, and is reminded of return; he then appears as the one who imposes an endpoint on someone else. The boundary-breaker becomes a self-appointed boundary-maker.
- trace:
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B004: Water settling where a flood reaches its endpoint gives the focus restraint a material image of forcing motion to stop.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Rising water and sweeping force supplies the actor's prior boundary-crossing motion.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B006: Visible appearance and mirror casts the claim of sufficiency as a self-image that organizes later conduct.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Self-sufficiency supplies the actor's imagined warrant to set another person's limit.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B001: Return to a prior point supplies the counter-direction to both overflow and self-appointed finality.

## seen watcher
- reading: Look at the watcher made visible: his attempt to police another is itself exposed within a knowing divine gaze.
- mechanism: The repeated seeing root moves from self-image, through interrogative inspection, to God's seeing. The focus no longer grants the prohibitor the position of sovereign watcher; it turns his act into a visible object nested inside a more comprehensive knowing gaze.
- trace:
  - 96:9 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B004: Mutual visibility and facing makes the focus question a scene in which observer and observed positions can reverse.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B006: The mirror and visible-appearance image supplies the actor's enclosed, self-authored field of vision.
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B001: Disclosure to a knower makes the prohibitor's conduct epistemically exposed rather than hidden.
  - 96:14 **ٱللَّهَ** ء ل ه B001: The worshipped deity identifies the source to which the culminating gaze belongs.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye or insight supplies the encompassing observation that catches the would-be controller within its field.

## prayer as rival obedience
- reading: Consider the one who intercepts a servant precisely in worship, trying to place his own command over the servant's directed obedience.
- mechanism: The next ayah supplies the withheld target: a servant at prayer. Worshipful obedience, guidance, command, and protection turn the prohibition into a jurisdictional conflict in which the restrainer attempts to override the servant's directed allegiance.
- trace:
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Restraint from an act anchors the conflict in the focus actor's attempt to stop another.
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Worship and submissive obedience identifies the stopped person through an allegiance already in operation.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B003: The specified worship act supplies the precise moment at which the prohibitor intervenes.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B001: Gentle direction toward the path and truth keeps open the possibility that the stopped servant is correctly oriented.
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: Request and obligation introduces a competing command relation rather than a merely private devotional act.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: Protection from harm supplies the constructive end of the command that the prohibitor may be obstructing.

## moral restraint inverted
- reading: Do not confuse prohibition with moral restraint: this actor may be stopping the person whose guidance and protective command reason should defend.
- mechanism: The conditional forks place guidance and protective conduct on one side, denial and turning away on the other. They prevent the surface title 'one who forbids' from carrying automatic moral authority: the apparent restrainer may be obstructing the very conduct that true reason would protect.
- trace:
  - 96:9 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B002: Reflective judgment turns the repeated question into an audit of competing moral descriptions.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B003: Reason that restrains from wrong supplies the legitimate moral function against which this prohibitor is tested.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B001: Direction toward path and truth supplies a positive orientation potentially possessed by the person being stopped.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: Protection from harm supplies the corrective conduct that a genuine moral restraint should preserve.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B001: Contradiction of truth supplies the negative alternative that strips the prohibitor's stance of epistemic authority.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Turning the back and withdrawing supplies the negative directional alternative opposed to guidance.

## forbidder who will not stop
- reading: See the one who makes another stop but will not stop himself, until cessation is imposed back upon him.
- mechanism: The same root returns with reversed valency: the person who makes another desist is warned that, if he does not desist, he will be forcibly seized. The would-be agent of stopping becomes the patient of an imposed stop, with lying and missing the right course attached to his forelock.
- trace:
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Transitive restraint establishes the actor as the person who tries to make someone else cease.
  - 96:15 **يَنتَهِ** ن ه ي B001: Ceasing and being restrained returns against that actor, reversing the focus operation onto its source.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Taking by the forelock supplies the external force that terminates the actor's failed self-restraint.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The forelock and seizure point localizes the reversal on the prohibitor's own body.
  - 96:16 **كَٰذِبَةٍ** ك ذ ب B001: Contradiction of truth diagnoses the actor whose command presented itself as authoritative.
  - 96:16 **خَاطِئَةٍ** خ ط ء B001: Missing what is right diagnoses the course of the person who presumed to terminate another's course.

## collective enforcement
- reading: The prohibitor emerges as the voice of a mobilized public faction, and his enforcement network is met by a counter-mobilization.
- mechanism: The singular prohibitor expands into a social enforcement system: he calls an assembly, that call is answered by a counter-call, and pushing or repelling force appears. The focus prohibition can therefore be heard as an act backed by audience, faction, and coercive capacity.
- trace:
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Restraint from an act supplies the individual command whose social machinery is later exposed.
  - 96:17 **فَلْيَدْعُ** د ع و B001: Calling and drawing others by speech supplies the mobilizing action behind the prohibitor's stance.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B003: The people's council and gathering supplies the public body from which the singular actor seeks backing.
  - 96:18 **سَنَدْعُ** د ع و B001: The answering call mirrors and overtakes the actor's attempted mobilization.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: Pushing, collision, and prevention supplies the coercive counter-force to the original act of prevention.

## stop reversed into approach
- reading: Refuse his claim to obedience: bowing down becomes the way to continue past his boundary and draw near.
- mechanism: The final sequence refuses obedience to the prohibitor and joins downward prostration to approach. His attempted endpoint is reversed: bodily lowering is not defeat or stoppage but the motion by which the servant continues and draws near.
- trace:
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B002: The endpoint imposed by the prohibitor supplies the trajectory that the final commands overturn.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B001: Obedient submission names the claim explicitly denied to the prohibitor.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B004: Bowing the head and bending supplies the downward bodily motion that continues despite the attempted stop.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Nearness rather than distance supplies the direction and result of refusing the prohibitor.

## sufficiency ends seeking
- reading: He presents himself as sufficient enough to end the other's seeking, making his own self-image the terminal horizon.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B005: Sufficiency that stops further seeking turns prohibition into a claim that no other source or path need be pursued.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B006: The mirror and visible appearance image supplies the self-authored perception behind that terminal claim.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Self-sufficiency supplies the explicit context pressure that activates the unusual sufficiency branch of the focus root.

## prohibition as public performance
- reading: Notice the man staging his prohibition before an assembly, while the performance itself remains visible to a gaze beyond that audience.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:9 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B005: Showing an act before people supplies the exploratory possibility that the prohibitor performs authority to be seen.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Restraint from an act anchors the performed gesture in the focus clause rather than in a free-standing spectacle theme.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B003: The council and gathering supplies the audience before whom prohibitive authority can be staged and reinforced.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: The culminating act of seeing reverses the performance by placing it under a gaze the actor does not control.


# Focus 96:10

## devoted identity
- reading: A person's servanthood comes into view at the moment it is bodily enacted as binding worship.
- mechanism: Submissive worship names the person's relation, while binding ritual prayer gives that relation an embodied event; servanthood becomes visible precisely when it is enacted.
- trace:
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Worship and submissive obedience make the noun an enacted devotional identity.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B001: Binding ritual prayer supplies the concrete act and bodily postures that manifest that identity.

## owned worshipper
- reading: The praying figure can also be a socially vulnerable, ownable person whose worship exceeds the relation imposed on him.
- mechanism: The phrase overlays external social possession and an interiorly directed ritual act. The same person can be socially ownable yet act within a devotional relation not exhausted by that status.
- trace:
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B001: Slavery and owned status preserve the vulnerable social sense of the noun.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B003: Prescribed worship gives the socially dependent person an action with its own normative order.

## supplicatory relation
- reading: The servant enters an address in which plea and praise move toward another and mercy can move back.
- mechanism: Prayer is relational traffic rather than posture alone: submissive address moves outward as plea or praise and opens toward blessing, mercy, or purification.
- trace:
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Devotional submission supplies the speaking relation of the worshipper.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B002: Supplication, praise, and mercy make prayer a bidirectional relation rather than a bare motion.

## dependent return
- reading: Prayer is the created dependent's repeated return from imagined sufficiency into attachment and nearness.
- mechanism: Creation and originating attachment establish dependence; imagined self-sufficiency breaks its recognition; return and final approach restore its direction. Prayer becomes a recurrent reattachment of the created servant rather than an isolated pious episode.
- trace:
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Submissive worship anchors the model in the servant's chosen dependence.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B001: Binding prayer supplies the recurrent embodied act of reattachment.
  - 96:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing, repairing, and completing supply an originating relation that sustains the servant.
  - 96:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B002: Creative bringing-into-being makes dependence prior to the servant's action.
  - 96:2 **عَلَقٍ** ع ل ق B001: Attachment and clinging provide the material image for original dependence.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Self-sufficiency supplies the counter-state that forgets attachment.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B001: Return restores direction toward the relation from which autonomy had turned.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Nearness gives the restored dependence a spatial outcome.

## contested allegiance
- reading: The worshipper selects an allegiance: prayer refuses a transgressive command while enacting servitude toward the Lord.
- mechanism: Lordship, transgressive prohibition, command, turning away, negated obedience, prostration, and approach form a contest over who may direct the servant. عبد no longer marks generic lowliness: it becomes selective allegiance enacted through prayer and refusal of a rival command.
- trace:
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Submissive obedience, including the inventory's possibility of misdirected obedience, makes allegiance the focus issue.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B001: Binding prayer is the concrete act through which allegiance is declared.
  - 96:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Lordship, ownership, and sovereignty establish one legitimate claim over the servant.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B001: Crossing the limit in disobedience characterizes the rival claim as an overreach.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Prohibition and restraint supply the rival's attempted control of the prayer act.
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: Demand and obligation make command itself an explicit axis of comparison.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Turning away supplies the directional refusal opposed to devotional approach.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B001: Obedience is explicitly negated toward the prohibitor, separating submission from indiscriminate compliance.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B001: Voluntary abasement supplies the positive bodily form of the servant's chosen allegiance.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Approach gives chosen submission a destination opposite to turning away.

## conditional diagnosis
- reading: The prayer is evidence inside a two-track diagnosis; guidance, protective command, denial, and withdrawal keep more than one agent reading live.
- mechanism: The focus act is followed by two conditional tracks: being upon guidance or commanding protection, versus denying and turning away. Prayer becomes the hinge of an inquiry whose agent assignments and moral diagnosis must be inferred rather than a label that automatically settles them.
- trace:
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Devotional obedience supplies the candidate identity being tested by the surrounding alternatives.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B003: Prescribed worship supplies the observable act around which the diagnosis branches.
  - 96:11 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The attention-seeking interrogative keeps the reader evaluating rather than merely receiving a settled report.
  - 96:11 **كَانَ** ك و ن B001: Occurrence and presence in a state supply the conditional possibility of being upon guidance.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B001: Gentle direction toward the right path supplies the first live moral track.
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: Command and obligation extend the first track from private state to directed action.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B002: Putting oneself in protection gives the commanded piety a protective mechanism.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B001: Contradiction of truth supplies the opposed diagnostic track.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Turning away gives the opposed track its bodily direction.

## embodied inscription
- reading: Prayer is embodied writing: the servant's learned allegiance becomes a visible mark that human and divine observers can read differently.
- mechanism: Reading, a visible naming-mark, teaching as a distinguishing sign, and the writing reed establish a semiotic field before the focus; seeing and the bodily trace of prostration continue it afterward. Prayer can therefore be read as knowledge written by the servant's body and made publicly legible.
- trace:
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Devotional service supplies the identity that the bodily sign expresses.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B001: Ritual postures turn the servant's relation into a bodily sequence capable of bearing a sign.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B002: Reading and recitation open the context as reception and performance of signs.
  - 96:1 **بِٱسْمِ** س م و B001: The non-dominant mapped image of a visible identifying mark makes naming materially legible.
  - 96:4 **عَلَّمَ** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing mark that guides supplies the bridge from teaching to visible orientation.
  - 96:4 **بِٱلْقَلَمِ** ق ل م B003: The sharpened writing reed supplies the explicit inscription instrument.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Sight and insight make the embodied inscription answerable to a witness.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B003: The organs and trace of prostration make worship leave a bodily mark.

## voluntary lowering
- reading: Prayer distinguishes chosen lowering, which produces nearness, from forced lowering, which tries to turn the body into a captured object.
- mechanism: Overflowing force and prohibition lead to threatened forelock seizure and pushing, while prayer leads to commanded bowing and nearness. The context separates voluntary self-lowering from externally imposed subjugation and gives the two motions opposite outcomes.
- trace:
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B005: Physical subduing and smoothing provide a material analogy for a body made yielding.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B001: Ritual prayer supplies voluntary postural discipline rather than imposed collapse.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Rising water and sweeping force supply the upward, boundary-breaking counter-motion.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Restraint supplies the first attempt to arrest the servant's chosen motion.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Seizing by the forelock turns control into threatened bodily force.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The forelock as a grasping point localizes imposed control at the body's leading edge.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: Pushing, collision, and prevention extend the coercive motion.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B004: Bowing the head supplies a chosen downward motion that answers threatened forelock seizure.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Nearness is the result of voluntary lowering rather than coercive capture.

## rival summons
- reading: The prayer initiates a contest of calls: solitary supplication stands opposite a recruited council and an answering coercive assembly.
- mechanism: Recitation and supplicatory prayer establish one address; the later parallel calls gather a council and counter-gather pushing agents. The apparently private prayer becomes the acoustic center of rival summons and rival assemblies.
- trace:
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Devotional obedience supplies the lone speaker's relational stance.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B002: Supplication and praise make prayer an address capable of standing against later calls.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B002: Reading and recitation open an acoustic-verbal field before the prayer.
  - 96:17 **فَلْيَدْعُ** د ع و B001: Calling and inclining others through speech launch the prohibitor's counter-summons.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B003: The people's council and assembly give the first counter-summons a social body.
  - 96:18 **سَنَدْعُ** د ع و B001: The repeated call answers the first summons in the same acoustic register.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: Pushing and prevention characterize the effective force gathered by the answering call.

## snare subjugation
- reading: Coercive power tries to convert the prayer occasion into a snare that manufactures its own servant, while refusal keeps devotion from becoming capture.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B004: Making someone slave-like supplies attempted subjugation as a process rather than a settled identity.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B005: Snares and traps make the prayer occasion imageable as the site of attempted capture.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Prohibition supplies the first closing movement of the proposed trap.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Forelock seizure turns figurative capture into an explicit bodily threat.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: Pushing and prevention supply the enforcement mechanism of capture.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B001: Negated obedience prevents attempted subjugation from becoming accepted servitude.

## perfume mortars
- reading: As a tightly contained material analogy, prayer pounds and refines the servant's offered substance until devotion becomes fragrant and spreadable.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B012: The named perfume mortar supplies one implement in a preparation process.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B008: The broad pounding stone supplies the matching working surface for perfume.
  - 96:1 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B010: Perfuming and coating with scent supply the product domain that exposes the focus-root convergence.

## following race
- reading: Prayer is prompt close-following: the servant takes position behind a precedence and advances toward nearness.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B009: Brief delay and modest haste make the servant's response prompt rather than static.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B006: The second horse following at the leader's flank supplies ordered close pursuit.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B014: Bringing a horse near in running gives the final approach the same kinetic domain.

## mercy heat fork
- reading: The prayer scene holds a thermal fork: the servant seeks mercy while coercive power answers with scorching exposure.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Devotional submission keeps the thermal polarity attached to the praying servant.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B002: Supplication, praise, and mercy supply the beneficent pole of prayer.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B003: Encountering fire and its heat supplies the adverse pole latent in the split root.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B003: Scorching fire and hot wind activate the adverse thermal pole in the threat sequence.


# Focus 96:11

## conditional adjudication
- reading: An alerting request to adjudicate a live hypothetical: suppose the person really occupied guidance; how must the case then be evaluated?
- mechanism: The utterance does not settle a fact; it recruits the addressee to judge a suspended case in which someone's being is tested against guidance.
- trace:
  - 96:11 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The tell-me and alert formula makes the opening a demand to consider the conditional case rather than a report of eyesight.
  - 96:11 **كَانَ** ك و ن B001: Being and temporal predication supply the deliberately unresolved state posited by the conditional.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward road or truth supplies the criterion against which that state is judged.

## occupied course
- reading: Guidance is an occupied and inspectable course: the question asks where the subject stands, how he is oriented, and what direction his conduct sustains.
- mechanism: Guidance becomes a position maintained upon a traversable course, not merely an inward property possessed by a person.
- trace:
  - 96:11 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye or insight asks the addressee to inspect the person's actual placement.
  - 96:11 **كَانَ** ك و ن B002: Place, position, or rank turns being into an occupied stance carried by عَلَى.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B002: Course, manner, and intended direction make guidance the trajectory on which the person is situated.

## composed bearing
- reading: It may also ask whether the subject's very bearing manifests a calm, non-routed form of guidance.
- mechanism: The conditional can test an observable manner of holding oneself, allowing guidance to appear as non-panicked steadiness as well as correct direction.
- trace:
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B010: Calm stillness and composed bearing give guidance a visible bodily and affective manner.
  - 96:11 **كَانَ** ك و ن B001: Being or occurring lets that composed manner function as the condition under examination.

## legible guidance
- reading: Read the person's course: guidance is a legible and teachable orientation whose effects can answer the conditional.
- mechanism: Reading, disclosure, and the writing implement turn the baseline act of adjudication into evidence-work: guidance is a direction whose marks can be gathered, taught, and inspected.
- trace:
  - 96:11 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B002: Reflective judgment anchors the focus question as an act of considered assessment.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B001: Clarifying a road or truth supplies the object that the surrounding knowledge practices make legible.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B002: Reading, recitation, and causing another to read establish active reception and articulation of signs.
  - 96:4 **عَلَّمَ** ع ل م B001: Disclosure of a thing to a knower makes hidden orientation available for judgment.
  - 96:4 **بِٱلْقَلَمِ** ق ل م B003: The sharpened writing stick gives disclosure a durable, examinable trace.

## dependence overflow return
- reading: Being on guidance means inhabiting dependence without mistaking support for autonomy: a bounded course that neither floods its limits nor forgets its return.
- mechanism: Attachment supplies creaturely dependence, self-seen independence releases boundary-crossing force, and return closes the circuit. Being upon guidance can therefore mean remaining rightly borne within relations of support and return.
- trace:
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B002: Course and intended direction anchor the focus in a trajectory that can remain ordered or exceed its bounds.
  - 96:2 **عَلَقٍ** ع ل ق B001: Clinging to and depending on another supplies the initial relational support denied by imagined self-sufficiency.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Rising water and sweeping force image transgression as energy that escapes a containing course.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Wealth and self-sufficiency supply the perceived release from dependence that precedes overflow.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B001: Returning or being restored to what was supplies the terminal reversal of claimed autonomy.

## contested regimes of seeing
- reading: The opening interrupts a self-confirming gaze and asks whose seeing can establish the truth of the subject's direction.
- mechanism: The same seeing root moves among self-appraisal, repeated demands that the addressee consider, and a final seeing beyond the actor. The focus question becomes a contest over which viewpoint can truthfully identify guidance.
- trace:
  - 96:11 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The tell-me formula summons the addressee into an explicit act of judgment.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B006: Visible appearance and mirror imagery make the actor's self-seeing a potentially closed reflective surface.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Self-sufficiency is the conclusion produced inside that self-appraisal.
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B001: Disclosure to a knower opposes the actor's private verdict with knowledge that is not confined to his viewpoint.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye or insight supplies the encompassing observation that closes the sequence of disputed perceptions.

## guided blocked servant
- reading: The obstructed servant may be the one standing on guidance and issuing protection-bearing direction, while the person able to stop him may lack authority.
- mechanism: Restraint is exercised against a socially subordinate worshipper, yet the conditional can place that obstructed person on guidance and extend his action into protective command. Visible power and normative authority are reversed.
- trace:
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B001: Guidance toward road or truth supplies authority that need not coincide with social dominance.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Forbidding and restraining supply the coercive act whose legitimacy the focus question reopens.
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Humble worship and obedience characterize the blocked person's action from within rather than by his rank.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B003: The specific act of worship makes the servant's submission concrete at the moment of restraint.
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: Request and obligation allow the apparently subordinate person to become a source of normative direction.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: Repelling harm by protection gives that commanded direction a protective rather than merely prohibitive function.

## charitable test of the blocker
- reading: The question provisionally asks whether even the prohibiter might claim a guidance-and-protection rationale, before the paired denial-and-retreat condition exposes the rival account.
- mechanism: Keeping the prohibiter as the subject makes the focus a deliberately favorable hypothesis placed beside the later adverse hypothesis. The discourse tests even an opponent's claim to guidance before naming denial and retreat.
- trace:
  - 96:11 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The alerting tell-me formula initiates a case test rather than presupposing the answer.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B001: Guidance toward truth supplies the favorable possibility temporarily granted to the prohibiter.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Forbidding identifies the nearby actor whose conduct is placed under both conditional tests.
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: Command or obligation extends the favorable hypothesis from inward guidance to outward direction.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: Protecting against harm specifies the beneficial end that such command would need to serve.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B001: Contradiction of truth supplies the adverse branch of the paired evaluation.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Turning away supplies the adverse actor's directional withdrawal after denial.

## forked orientation
- reading: Guidance is the positive branch of a behavioral fork: truthful targeting and maintained orientation stand opposite falsification, turning away, and missing the mark.
- mechanism: Guidance as course is sharpened by a three-part contrary vector: falsifying the true description, turning away, and failing to hit the right mark. The condition asks about sustained orientation, not a static badge.
- trace:
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B002: Course, aim, and intended direction establish guidance as a vector of conduct.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B001: Opposition to truth corrupts the account by which the actor's direction is named.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Turning the back and withdrawing gives falsehood an enacted direction away.
  - 96:16 **خَاطِئَةٍ** خ ط ء B001: Missing the correct point or passing beyond it renders error as failed targeting.

## leading edge reversal
