- reading: To be on guidance may be to have one's leading edge rightly directed: coercion grabs the false front, while chosen prostration reorients the guided body toward nearness.
- mechanism: A focus branch makes guidance a leading front; the later forelock becomes the contested control point. Forced seizure of the false front is then answered by voluntary lowering of the head and approach.
- trace:
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B003: The first or leading front part lets guidance register as what heads and steers a moving body.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Taking by the forelock turns directional control into a concrete act of seizure.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The forelock and grasping it locate the contest at the body's leading edge.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B004: Bowing the head supplies a voluntary redirection of the same leading edge.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Nearness makes that voluntary lowering terminate in approach rather than defeat.

## rival summons
- reading: The conditional asks where a person truly stands when public councils and coercive responders can both be summoned; mobilization itself does not establish guidance.
- mechanism: Call and counter-call externalize two rival alignments: one invokes an assembled social circle, the other agents of repulsion. The focus conditional becomes a legitimacy test that crowd and force cannot answer by themselves.
- trace:
  - 96:11 **كَانَ** ك و ن B002: Position or rank anchors the question in where the subject stands amid competing alignments.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B002: Course and intended direction provide a criterion distinct from the size or force of an alliance.
  - 96:17 **فَلْيَدْعُ** د ع و B001: Calling and drawing others by speech initiate the public attempt to reinforce one side.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B003: The people's council or assembly gives that call a social body and visible prestige.
  - 96:18 **سَنَدْعُ** د ع و B001: The answering call mirrors the first summons and exposes the scene as competing mobilization.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: Pushing, striking, and preventing supply the counter-force that can break the first assembly's coercive confidence.

## descent into nearness
- reading: Being upon guidance is enacted through correctly allocated obedience: the guided person lowers himself without capitulating and, paradoxically, descent becomes approach.
- mechanism: The final commands enact the focus predicate through selective allegiance: refusing submission to the blocker, voluntarily lowering oneself, and thereby approaching. Guidance is composed action whose downward movement produces nearness.
- trace:
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B010: Calm stillness and composed bearing give the guided stance resilience under command and threat.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B001: Submission and obedience identify the allegiance explicitly refused toward the prohibiter.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B001: Lowering and humbling supply the positively chosen posture that replaces obedience to coercion.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Drawing near makes approach the spatial result of chosen lowering.

## recited case transmission
- reading: Exploratorily: have you received and rehearsed the report of this case well enough to transmit its test of direction?
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:11 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B003: Relaying reports and poetry recasts the interrogative act as receiving, retaining, and passing on a narrated case.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B002: Course and intended direction provide the content whose trajectory the report asks the hearer to assess.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B002: Reading and recitation activate an oral reception channel for the focus case.
  - 96:4 **بِٱلْقَلَمِ** ق ل م B003: The writing implement supplies the complementary durable channel by which the case can be preserved.

## protected hady servant
- reading: The vulnerable servant may simultaneously be the protected party: restraint does not cancel, and may expose, his inviolable standing on guidance.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B007: The protected person or captive called hady lets the focus predicate carry an echo of inviolability under constraint.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Forbidding and restraint supply the act that places the person's freedom of worship under pressure.
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B001: Bondage and ownership sharpen the social vulnerability carried by the word servant.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: Repelling harm by protection activates the focus branch's latent claim of inviolability.

## guidance as regulated watercourse
- reading: Guidance channels force toward sufficiency and return, while transgression is the same vital movement swollen into boundary-breaking overflow.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:11 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B001: Quenching and freedom from thirst introduce a form-distant image of water reaching sufficiency.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B002: Course and intended direction give that water image a channel and destination.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Rising water and sweeping force provide the contrary image of flow exceeding its bounds.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B006: Returning rain or water supplies recirculation after overflow rather than autonomous escape.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B005: Moisture, rain, and dampness keep the water image latent even inside the later social confrontation.


# Focus 96:12

## directive as self protection
- reading: Or issued a directive by which the hearer actively places the self under protective caution.
- mechanism: A directive transfers an obligation to the hearer, while taqwa names the hearer's active placement of the self behind a guard. The construction therefore joins outward speech to inward risk regulation.
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: The command-or-directive image supplies an obligating speech act and functions as the initiating force of the mechanism.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B002: The image of placing oneself in protective caution supplies the commanded content and locates its work inside the hearer's conduct.

## authority measured by shelter
- reading: Or exercised command in a way whose defining result was protection rather than domination.
- mechanism: The command root can activate office and command-holding, but the object prevents authority from being self-validating: its legitimate tendency here is to ward harm away. Authority is read functionally, by what it shelters.
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B003: The authority-and-command-holder image supplies a vertical social relation whose legitimacy is being tested.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: The protective-barrier image supplies shelter from damage and functions as the criterion imposed on authority.

## deliberated guarding
- reading: Or formed and transmitted a considered resolve toward self-guarding.
- mechanism: The utterance can faintly activate counsel, inward consultation, and formed intent alongside ordinary command. On that branch, taqwa is not merely imposed from outside; it is a protective resolve considered and accepted.
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B007: The consultation-and-deliberation image supplies considered intent and functions as an inward echo of the outward command.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B002: The self-guarding image gives the deliberation a concrete behavioral end: voluntarily taking protective caution.

## recitation to protective formation
- reading: Or completed a process of recitation and teaching by forming the hearer into protective self-governance.
- mechanism: The opening sequence turns the focus command into the culmination of a formative process: material is gathered and recited, a lord nurtures it toward completion, knowledge is disclosed, and a prepared writing tool transmits it. Commanding taqwa is thus pedagogical formation of a protective capacity, not a detached order.
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: The directive image remains the focus speech act but now functions as one stage in an instructional process.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B002: The self-protective caution image supplies the disposition that the instructional process is inferred to form.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B002: The reading-and-recitation image supplies repeated vocal transmission and functions as the first formative channel.
  - 96:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: The nurture-repair-completion image supplies sustained formation and makes the command developmental rather than merely coercive.
  - 96:4 **عَلَّمَ** ع ل م B001: The disclosure-to-a-knower image supplies cognitive unveiling and functions as the command's epistemic preparation.
  - 96:4 **بِٱلْقَلَمِ** ق ل م B003: The pared writing-stick image supplies durable inscription and functions as a material carrier of instruction.

## cultivated protection
- reading: Or cultivated a guarded mode of life whose limits allow it to grow well.
- mechanism: Growth, measured formation, and good vegetation activate a cultivation model inside the focus roots: command tends a capacity until it flourishes, while taqwa protects that growth. Restraint and increase become mutually enabling rather than opposed.
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B004: The growth-and-blessing image supplies increase within the command root and functions as the cultivated outcome.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: The protective-barrier image supplies preservation from damage and functions as the condition under which growth can continue.
  - 96:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: The nurture-and-completion image supplies tending over time and links authority to development.
  - 96:1 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: The measuring-and-proportioning image supplies shaped limits and functions as disciplined formation.
  - 96:3 **ٱلْأَكْرَمُ** ك ر م B002: The good-vegetation-and-rain image supplies flourishing and makes the protective limit productive rather than merely restrictive.

## boundary against overflow
- reading: Or commanded an inner retaining boundary against the overflow produced by imagined self-sufficiency.
- mechanism: The human's imagined self-sufficiency removes felt dependence and releases boundary-crossing like water rising beyond its limit. The announced return restores an outer horizon. Taqwa becomes the inner barrier that checks overflow before return imposes the boundary from outside.
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: The directive image supplies an intervention and functions as the call to restore a breached limit.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: The harm-warding barrier supplies containment and functions as the internal check on overflow.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: The rising-water and sweeping-force image supplies uncontrolled excess and functions as the danger the barrier must contain.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: The self-sufficiency image supplies the perceived absence of need and functions as the condition that disables voluntary restraint.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B001: The return-to-what-was image supplies an unavoidable reversal and functions as the outer boundary denied by self-sufficiency.

## praying servant becomes guardian
- reading: Or the praying servant's guided worship became an outward summons into protective self-governance.
- mechanism: On the reading that the praying servant remains the subject of being on guidance and commanding taqwa, embodied worship does not terminate in private devotion. It generates speech that invites others into self-protection; the prohibited servant becomes a protective guide.
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: The directive image supplies outward address and functions as the servant's social extension of worship.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B002: The self-guarding image supplies the content shared with others and makes the servant's action protective.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: The prohibition-and-restraint image supplies the hostile speech act against which the servant's protective directive is contrasted.
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: The worship-and-submissive-obedience image identifies the servant's vertical allegiance and functions as the source of his alternative conduct.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B003: The specific-worship image supplies embodied prayer and functions as the act from which guidance and protective speech radiate.
  - 96:11 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The attention-and-inquiry image supplies an open evaluative question and keeps the pronoun assignment under examination.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B001: The gentle indication toward road and truth supplies orientation and functions as the path that issues in commanding protection.

## prohibitor faces authority test
- reading: Or proved his claim to command by directing power toward protection rather than obstructing worship.
- mechanism: On the competing pronoun reading, the one who prohibits is tested by counterfactual alternatives: is his exercise of command guided and protective, or does it deny truth and turn away? The focus is then an audit of command itself, distinguishing protective authority from obstructive control.
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B003: The authority-and-command-holder image supplies office-like power and functions as the social capacity under examination.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: The protective-barrier image supplies the positive criterion by which an authority's command is tested.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: The forbidding-and-checking image supplies the observed use of power and functions as the act placed on trial.
  - 96:11 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The interrogative-attention image supplies explicit examination and functions as the frame for testing the prohibitor.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B001: The gentle guidance-to-the-road image supplies the first positive criterion paired with protective command.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B001: The contrary-to-truth image supplies the negative alternative and functions as corruption of authoritative judgment.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: The turning-away image supplies withdrawal from truth and completes the negative alternative to protective authority.

## guarding under seen accountability
- reading: Or called for self-guarding sustained by the awareness that conduct is already disclosed and seen.
- mechanism: Knowledge and divine seeing relocate the protective barrier from external surveillance to internalized accountability. Taqwa becomes conduct regulated before compulsion because the actor understands the act to be disclosed and seen.
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: The directive image supplies the articulated norm and functions as the external statement to be internalized.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B002: The self-placing-in-caution image supplies internal regulation and functions as conduct maintained without immediate force.
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B001: The disclosure-to-the-knower image supplies awareness and functions as the epistemic condition of accountability.
  - 96:14 **ٱللَّهَ** ء ل ه B001: The worship-and-deity image identifies the vertical relation within which being seen acquires normative force.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: The seeing-by-eye-or-insight image supplies observation and functions as the horizon within which self-guarding operates.

## self restraint before imposed restraint
- reading: Or opened the possibility of self-restraint before unchecked conduct is stopped by restraint from outside.
- mechanism: The sequence offers two ways for harmful motion to stop. Taqwa is a self-installed guard responsive to command; failure to stop leads to seizure by the forelock and agents characterized by pushing and prevention. The focus becomes the still-open interval for voluntary restraint before restraint is imposed.
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: The directive image supplies a non-physical intervention and functions as the opportunity for voluntary response.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: The protective barrier supplies self-restraint and functions as the alternative to being physically checked.
  - 96:15 **يَنتَهِ** ن ه ي B001: The stopping-and-restraining image supplies the unmet demand to cease and marks the transition toward compulsion.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: The taking-by-the-forelock image supplies bodily seizure and functions as externally imposed control.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The forelock-and-grasp image locates external restraint on the body and makes loss of agency concrete.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: The pushing, collision, and prevention image supplies enforcement and functions as the terminal counterpart to voluntary guarding.

## competing social mobilizations
- reading: Or used authority to create protection, the quality that separates guardianship from a faction's ability to summon force.
- mechanism: The later exchange of calls and collectives turns the focus command into a contest between social powers. One side can summon its assembly; the counter-call summons preventing force. Against both, commanding taqwa names authority by its protective end, not by the size of the group it can mobilize.
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B003: The authority-and-command-holder image supplies organized power and functions as the focus of the social comparison.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: The protective-barrier image supplies the criterion that distinguishes guardianship from mere mobilizing power.
  - 96:17 **فَلْيَدْعُ** د ع و B001: The calling-and-verbal-drawing image supplies the first act of mobilization and functions as the challenger's social reach.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B003: The council-and-assembly image supplies an organized peer group and functions as the challenger's claimed support.
  - 96:18 **سَنَدْعُ** د ع و B001: The repeated calling image supplies the answering mobilization and creates a mirrored contest of summons.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: The pushing-and-preventing image supplies coercive capacity and functions as the force behind the counter-summons.

## protective noncompliance enables nearness
- reading: Or commanded a protective refusal of false obedience that makes prostration and nearness possible.
- mechanism: The closing sequence resolves taqwa as selective allegiance rather than generalized timidity. A protective barrier is placed against obedience to the obstructing human; bodily lowering redirects submission, and that refusal enables approach. Guarding and nearness become consecutive motions.
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: The directive image supplies a norm that must be distinguished from the rival human demand.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: The protective-barrier image supplies resistance to harmful compliance and functions as the condition of redirected movement.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B001: The obedience-and-submission image supplies the rejected relation and makes taqwa a filter over whom one follows.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B001: The lowering-and-humbling image supplies embodied submission and functions as the positive redirection of allegiance.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: The drawing-near image supplies forward movement and functions as the result enabled by protective noncompliance.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B005: The offering-and-nearness-to-God image specifies the relational destination of the approach.

## taqwa as legible mark
- reading: Or established self-guarding as the legible mark by which a guided orientation can be recognized.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B005: The mark, sign, or appointed-time image supplies legibility within the focus root and functions as the proposed public trace of the command.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B002: The self-protective caution image supplies the inward disposition whose outward pattern is inferred to become recognizable.
  - 96:1 **بِٱسْمِ** س م و B001: The visible branding-mark image supplies an identifying trace and functions as the contextual activator of legibility.
  - 96:4 **عَلَّمَ** ع ل م B002: The distinguishing trace that guides toward a thing supplies recognition and functions as a second sign-like support.

## guarded gait toward nearness
- reading: Or instructed a guarded gait: movement adjusted to vulnerability so that approach can continue without ruin.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: The directive image supplies guidance for conduct and functions as an instruction to alter how one moves.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B003: The guarded gait prompted by hoof pain supplies vulnerable, terrain-sensitive movement and functions as the core material analogy.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B001: The gentle indication toward a road supplies orientation and gives the guarded gait a traversable path.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: The drawing-near image supplies destination-directed motion and shows that caution need not end in withdrawal.

## hydraulic restraining basin
- reading: Or called for a protective retaining basin within the self, receiving and stopping the flood of excess before it sweeps onward.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: The directive image supplies the act that calls containment into operation.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: The protective-barrier image supplies the retaining structure and functions as defense against destructive flow.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: The water rising with sweeping force supplies the excess flow and functions as the pressure requiring containment.
  - 96:15 **يَنتَهِ** ن ه ي B004: The basin where floodwater settles at the torrent's end supplies collected arrest and functions as the material analogue of a limit.


# Focus 96:13

## inquiry denial withdrawal
- reading: Consider the consequential possibility that active falsification completes itself as withdrawal.
- mechanism: The opening does not simply report misconduct; it recruits the addressee to consider a possibility. Form-II denial actively assigns falsehood, and the coordinated withdrawal makes that epistemic act culminate in disengagement.
- trace:
  - 96:13 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The alerting tell-me formula turns the clause into a diagnostic question rather than a flat visual report.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B002: Attributing falsehood supplies an active repudiation, not merely the speaker's own untruth.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Turning away converts the preceding verdict into bodily, attentional, or practical refusal.

## visible counterfacing
- reading: An observable face-to-face test in which contradiction is followed by a visible reorientation away.
- mechanism: Seeing places parties within an inspectable field; denial contests what is presented there; turning the face changes one's orientation without erasing the encounter. The ayah can therefore stage evasion under observation rather than a purely interior opinion.
- trace:
  - 96:13 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B004: Mutual visibility and facing supply an encounter in which the conduct can be watched.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B001: Contradiction of truth supplies the contested content inside the visible encounter.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B006: Turning or directing the face makes orientation itself the mechanism by which the encounter is accepted or evaded.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Withdrawal selects the evasive direction within the broader face-orientation image.

## denial assumes charge
- reading: The denier's verdict can become administrative: he falsifies, then takes charge.
- mechanism: If the governance branch remains live, denial does not end in absence but advances into control: a judgment that marks something false is followed by assuming authority over what happens next. This coexists with, rather than replaces, the ordinary withdrawal reading.
- trace:
  - 96:13 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B002: Reflective judgment supplies the evaluative arena in which the denial is made.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B002: Assigning falsehood supplies the delegitimating act that can precede control.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B003: Taking charge of an affair changes the endpoint from private retreat to exercised authority.

## knowledge chain refusal
- reading: The denial is an attempted break in a gathered, taught, and inscribed chain of knowing.
- mechanism: Repeated reading gathers material; teaching discloses what was not known; the pen stabilizes that disclosure. Against this upstream chain, the focus pair becomes a counter-process: what has been assembled and made knowable is actively marked false and then abandoned.
- trace:
  - 96:13 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The alerting inquiry summons an assessment of what happens to the preceding knowledge process.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B001: Gathering and assembly make reading an upstream collection of signs into a coherent whole.
  - 96:5 **عَلَّمَ يَعْلَمْ** ع ل م B001: Disclosure to a knower supplies the transition from prior nonknowledge to intelligibility.
  - 96:4 **بِٱلْقَلَمِ** ق ل م B003: The trimmed writing instrument gives the disclosed knowledge a transmissible and durable medium.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B002: Active falsification interrupts the authority of the assembled and transmitted material.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Withdrawal completes the interruption by refusing continued attention or participation.

## dependency denied as autonomy
- reading: Denial and turning away perform an impossible autonomy: the attached creature declares independence, overflows, and tries to sever the relation to which it returns.
- mechanism: The human begins as created attachment, then self-perception of sufficiency licenses overflow beyond a limit, while return remains unavoidable. The focus denial can thus enact a false declaration of independence, and turning away attempts to sever a dependence that the surrounding sequence keeps in force.
- trace:
  - 96:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B002: Creative origination supplies a received beginning rather than self-production.
  - 96:2 **عَلَقٍ** ع ل ق B001: Attachment and clinging make dependence material at the human origin.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Overflowing water supplies a force that exceeds its containing boundary.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B002: Reflective judgment makes perceived self-sufficiency an interpretation of the self, not a neutral fact.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Self-sufficiency supplies the imagined release from dependence.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B001: Return to a prior destination bounds the autonomy claim with an unavoidable counter-movement.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B001: Contradiction of truth becomes the verbal enactment of the false independence claim.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Turning away gives the attempted severance a spatial and behavioral form.

## enclosed sightlines
- reading: The inquiry turns the denier into the observed object: he may turn away, but he cannot turn himself out of sight.
- mechanism: The repeated inquiry moves seeing among the self, the addressee, and finally God. The focus actor can turn away from an addressed field of attention, but the next ayah reverses visual agency: the one considered is also seen. Withdrawal changes direction, not visibility.
- trace:
  - 96:13 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B004: Mutual visibility supplies a field in which changing one's facing cannot abolish the other sightline.
  - 96:9 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The earlier alerting formula begins the repeated summons to inspect conduct.
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B001: Knowledge as disclosure makes the final seeing epistemically consequential rather than merely optical.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye or insight shifts the observing subject and encloses the attempted withdrawal.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Turning away is recast as movement within, rather than escape from, the field of observation.

## guidance denial fork
- reading: The ayah occupies one arm of a live test: direction that protects others versus falsification that turns away.
- mechanism: The preceding conditional offers gentle direction and protective command; the focus conditional offers falsification and withdrawal. Read together, they form a fork of orientations and effects rather than a self-contained accusation, while leaving open whether one subject is tested under alternatives or different roles are contrasted.
- trace:
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies the positive directional alternative.
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: Request and obligation turn orientation into conduct that can be extended to another.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: Ward against harm gives the commanded conduct a protective social effect.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B002: Assigning falsehood supplies the negative epistemic alternative to guidance.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Withdrawal supplies the negative directional alternative to being led along a path.

## restrainer becomes restrained
- reading: The pair marks a governing bodily direction: the restrainer's false course is reversed, stopped at its leading edge, and made answerable.
- mechanism: The one who restrains another is later required to reach an endpoint, and the threatened grasp targets the body's leading edge. The focus is the hinge: verbal falsification and voluntary withdrawal are recoded as an embodied direction that can itself be stopped, seized, and characterized as false and culpably off-course.
- trace:
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Restraint from an act establishes the first direction of control.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B002: Falsification supplies the speech-act hinge between controlling another and being countered.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Withdrawal gives the culpable orientation a bodily trajectory.
  - 96:15 **يَنتَهِ** ن ه ي B002: Reaching an endpoint reverses the root's earlier restraint back onto the restrainer as a demand to cease.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Taking by the forelock supplies the counter-force that arrests the attempted trajectory.
  - 96:16 **نَاصِيَةٍ** ن ص ي B001: The forelock localizes agency at the body's front or leading edge.
  - 96:16 **كَٰذِبَةٍ** ك ذ ب B001: Falsehood reappears as a quality of that embodied leading edge, extending the focus act into character.
  - 96:16 **خَاطِئَةٍ** خ ط ء B002: Culpable wrong adds intentional moral deviation to the embodied falsehood.

## alliance mobilization
- reading: The denier's turn can be a regrouping maneuver: falsification becomes a public claim, and patronage converts withdrawal into mobilization.
- mechanism: Calling an assembly and answering with a force of pushing and prevention reveals organized sides. This activates the focus authority and alliance branches: turning away need not be a solitary disappearance but can be a pivot from direct encounter toward one's council, with denial functioning as the claim around which support is mobilized.
- trace:
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B002: Assigning falsehood supplies the delegitimating claim around which a side can be organized.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B004: Alliance, support, and patronage make the turn socially connective rather than merely evasive.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B003: Taking charge supplies a leadership role within the mobilized side.
  - 96:17 **فَلْيَدْعُ** د ع و B001: Vocal calling supplies the act that recruits a side into the conflict.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B003: The council or gathering supplies the denier's available social body.
  - 96:18 **سَنَدْعُ** د ع و B001: The answering call mirrors recruitment and establishes a counter-side.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: Pushing, collision, and prevention give the counter-assembly a functional force.

## refusal descent approach
- reading: Turning away exerts a copyable social direction, but refusal of obedience, bodily descent, and approach break and reverse it.
- mechanism: The closing commands answer the focus trajectory point by point: do not align your will with his, lower the body's front, and move near. The denier's turning away becomes a directional pressure that could propagate through obedience, while the commanded response breaks that succession and substitutes descent-plus-approach.
- trace:
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B002: Succession without a break makes one orientation potentially reproducible by a follower.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Withdrawal supplies the trajectory that the final imperatives counter.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B001: Obedient compliance identifies the channel through which another person's orientation could be copied.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B004: Bowing the head gives the counter-response a concrete downward bodily vector.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Drawing near supplies the opposite spatial endpoint to turning away.

## turning bounded by return
- reading: Its terminality is weakened: the turn away is a temporary vector bounded by unavoidable return and an available movement near.
- mechanism: Return to the sovereign source appears before the focus, and commanded nearness appears after it. These two vectors bound the turn away, weakening any reading of تولّى as a final or successful exit: it is a local orientation inside a larger return-and-approach frame.
- trace:
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Sovereignty and possession identify the pole toward which return is directed.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B001: Return to what was supplies a larger counter-vector before the focus withdrawal.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Turning away supplies the local vector whose finality is being tested.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Drawing near closes the context with a chosen counter-vector.

## failed charge and lookback
- reading: As a contained kinetic analogy, the ayah asks the observer to notice a failed advance: resolve breaks, looks back, and retreats under counter-force.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:13 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing supplies the observer who registers the change from advance to retreat.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B004: A charge that fails to hold supplies the first breakdown in forward motion.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B007: Running and then stopping to look behind supplies the pivot between failed advance and withdrawal.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Turning away completes the kinetic pattern as retreat.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B005: Pursuit in the later threat keeps the failed-advance image dynamically connected to the context.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: Pushing and collision supply the counter-force before which the attempted charge fails.

## hydraulic false sufficiency
- reading: As a contained water analogy, apparent saturation overflows into autonomy, its expected supply fails, and returning or following water exposes dependence.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:13 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B001: The split-root quenching image supplies apparent saturation at the opening of the focus sequence.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B006: Milk expected to continue but drying up supplies a concrete failure of presumed sufficiency.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B010: Rain following an earlier rain supplies succession after the failed supply.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Overflowing water makes excess, rather than lack, the immediate condition of transgression.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Self-sufficiency supplies the human interpretation placed upon temporary saturation.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B006: Returning rain or water makes supply cyclical and received rather than autonomously possessed.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B005: Dew, rain, and moisture extend the contextual water field without determining the focus lexemes.

## surface that lies
- reading: As a contained visual model, the actor's presented surface itself makes a false claim, and turning the face attempts to manage an inspection that later reads the body's front.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:13 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B006: Visible appearance and mirror supply a surface offered for inspection.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B009: A colored garment that lies by its condition materializes falsehood as misleading presentation.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B006: Turning the face supplies an attempt to control which aspect remains visible.
  - 96:1 **بِٱسْمِ** س م و B002: A visible sign from which a viewer reads condition makes surface evidence contextually active.
  - 96:4 **بِٱلْقَلَمِ** ق ل م B003: The writing instrument supplies deliberate inscription as a contrast to deceptive appearance.
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing mark that guides recognition makes the inspected body or surface legible.
  - 96:16 **نَاصِيَةٍ** ن ص ي B001: The forelock brings visible bodily frontage into the later attribution of falsehood.


# Focus 96:14

## epistemic exposure
- reading: The question exposes a practical contradiction: the subject can know that he is seen yet act as though that knowledge never entered conduct.
- mechanism: Knowledge as disclosure and sight as ocular or inward perception form a reflexive accountability loop: the subject is asked whether what should be clear to him includes the fact that he is already perceptible to another.
- trace:
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B001: Knowledge as a thing disclosed to the knower supplies the human recognition demanded by the question.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye or insight supplies the encompassing perception to which the subject's knowledge must answer.

## marked legibility
- reading: The act marks and displays its agent; Allah's seeing reads an evidence-bearing appearance rather than merely penetrating a secret.
- mechanism: A mark makes something identifiable and directs an observer toward it; sight receives that marked appearance. The verse can therefore present conduct not merely as privately known but as becoming its own visible evidence.
- trace:
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B002: The distinguishing mark that points to a thing turns knowledge into legibility and makes conduct evidentiary.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B006: Visible appearance and mirror imagery supply the field in which the subject's own marks return to view.

## audience displacement
- reading: The verse asks why the subject lets a lesser audience govern conduct while ignoring the worshipped witness already seeing it.
- mechanism: The focus can stage a competition between audiences. If conduct is adjusted for human spectators, the question displaces that social gaze with the gaze of the one to whom worship is due.
- trace:
  - 96:14 **ٱللَّهَ** ء ل ه B001: Worship and the worshipped one establish the authority of the audience named as seer.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B005: Showing an act before people supplies a rival audience whose approval can be displaced by divine sight.

## deliberative verdict
- reading: Allah's seeing is carried as observation already tending toward deliberation and verdict.
- mechanism: Sight can be carried forward into evaluation: what is seen is not only registered but weighed. The non-dominant mapped root strengthens a judicial shade without replacing the focus verb's ordinary visual anchor.
- trace:
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B002: Reflective opinion and judgment make seeing capable of issuing in an assessment.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B004: The non-dominant image of considered deliberation adds a cautious sense of sight becoming a weighed verdict.
  - 96:14 **ٱللَّهَ** ء ل ه B002: The fixed divine name identifies whose seeing can carry authoritative evaluative force.

## taught capacity self indicts
- reading: A creature equipped to read, learn, and inscribe is convicted by its own taught capacity when it fails to let divine seeing govern action.
- mechanism: Repeated reading, teaching, and the writing instrument make knowledge an imparted capacity with a material trace. When يَعْلَم returns in the focus, the question sounds less like a request for missing information and more like an indictment generated by the very faculty the subject received.
- trace:
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B001: Disclosed knowledge anchors the focus question in an epistemic capacity already available to the subject.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Divine perception names the fact that the taught capacity should disclose.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B002: Reading and recitation begin the sequence as an exercised mode of receiving and voicing knowledge.
  - 96:3 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B002: The repeated reading image makes instruction persistent rather than incidental.
  - 96:4 **عَلَّمَ** ع ل م B001: Teaching as disclosure supplies the giver-side origin of the knowing later demanded.
  - 96:5 **عَلَّمَ يَعْلَمْ** ع ل م B001: The paired teaching and knowing occurrences frame human knowledge as movement out of prior non-disclosure.
  - 96:4 **بِٱلْقَلَمِ** ق ل م B003: The pared writing stick gives instruction a durable, inspectable medium and supports the later theme of legibility.

## dependency against self sufficiency
- reading: The watched subject's wrongdoing is the performance of an impossible independence: one formed in attachment tries to act outside the relation that still sees and contains him.
- mechanism: Measured creation and attachment establish dependent origin; transgression as boundary-crossing or overflowing force follows a self-assessment of sufficiency. The focus question exposes this autonomy as an epistemic severing from relations that still hold.
- trace:
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B001: Knowledge as disclosure anchors the contradiction between actual dependence and the subject's practical self-conception.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Divine sight supplies the encompassing relation that the claim of autonomy fails to escape.
  - 96:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B002: Bringing creation into being supplies a received rather than self-originating existence.
  - 96:2 **عَلَقٍ** ع ل ق B001: Attachment and clinging make dependency the material counter-image to imagined self-sufficiency.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B001: Crossing the limit in disobedience gives the false autonomy its behavioral effect.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Overflowing water and sweeping force materialize self-expansion beyond a containing boundary.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Self-sufficiency names the mistaken independence that triggers the overflow.

## recursive gaze reversal
- reading: The line overturns the subject's self-seeing: the one who pronounced himself sufficient is himself contained in the sight he failed to include.
- mechanism: The root of seeing moves from self-appraisal, through repeated alerting questions addressed to a witness, to Allah as seer. The focus is therefore the reversal point where the subject's verdict about himself becomes an object inside a larger gaze.
- trace:
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B001: Disclosed knowledge asks whether the subject recognizes the reversal from seeing himself to being seen.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye or insight anchors the final and encompassing side of the gaze reversal.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B002: Reflective judgment makes the subject's seeing of himself a self-authored verdict rather than neutral perception.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Self-sufficiency supplies the content of the subject's self-verdict.
  - 96:9 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The tell-me alerting formula turns seeing outward and recruits the addressee to inspect the scene.
  - 96:11 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The repeated alert renews inspection while testing an alternative account of the act.
  - 96:13 **أَرَءَيْتَ** ر ء ي B013: The third alert completes the human witnessing sequence immediately before divine sight is named.

## witness over rival authority
- reading: Allah witnesses a struggle over who may command worship, and the question voids the coercer's claim to act as the scene's highest authority.
- mechanism: Prohibition confronts worship, while guidance and protective command are set against denial and turning away. In that contest of directives, Allah's seeing functions as the superior witness that makes one party's attempted authority answerable to another jurisdiction.
- trace:
  - 96:14 **ٱللَّهَ** ء ل ه B001: The worshipped one supplies the higher authority whose sight evaluates the attempted prohibition.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B002: Reflective judgment lets divine seeing operate as assessment within the authority conflict.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: Restraint and prohibition supply the coercive command whose legitimacy is at issue.
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Submissive worship identifies the relation the prohibitor attempts to interrupt.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B003: The specified worship practice gives the prohibited submission a concrete enacted form.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B001: Gentle direction toward path and truth supplies the possible right orientation of the prohibited actor.
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: Demand and obligation introduce a competing command aligned with protection rather than obstruction.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: Repelling harm by protection gives the alternative command its safeguarding purpose.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B002: Attributing falsehood supplies the rejection by which the rival authority defends itself.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: Turning away and retreating complete the rejecting side of the authority conflict.

## body becomes evidence
- reading: Divine sight makes the wrongdoer self-disclosing: lie and error become a visible mark that can be named and physically apprehended.
- mechanism: The focus roots already allow a mark read through visible appearance. The following seizure of the forelock and its predicates of lying and missing the right transform moral concealment into an embodied, graspable exhibit.
- trace:
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing mark supplies the evidentiary transition from knowing to identifying the agent.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B006: Visible appearance and mirror imagery provide the surface on which moral traits become readable.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Taking by the forelock converts observed liability into physical apprehension at a visible bodily point.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The forelock and seizure image identifies the bodily locus where the seen agent becomes graspable.
  - 96:16 **نَاصِيَةٍ** ن ص ي B001: The repeated forelock holds the bodily exhibit in view while predicates are attached to it.
  - 96:16 **كَٰذِبَةٍ** ك ذ ب B001: The contrary-to-truth image labels the seized exterior with the agent's epistemic failure.
  - 96:16 **خَاطِئَةٍ** خ ط ء B001: Missing the right target makes error directional and completes the body's evidentiary description.

## counter summons public exposure
- reading: The focus exposes a bid for collective power and marks the moment when a local summons becomes visible within a stronger counter-summons.
- mechanism: Gathering and recitation at the opening are answered near the close by two opposed calls: one summons an assembly, the other summons agents of pushing and prevention. Allah's seeing becomes the hinge that turns attempted local mobilization into an exposed public confrontation.
- trace:
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B004: Mutual visibility and facing supply the exposed confrontation into which the competing summons develop.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B001: Gathering and collection establish an opening social image later echoed by rival mobilizations.
  - 96:17 **فَلْيَدْعُ** د ع و B001: Calling by raised speech supplies the subject's attempt to mobilize support.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B003: The council or gathered assembly gives the first call a concrete social body.
  - 96:18 **سَنَدْعُ** د ع و B001: The answering call mirrors and overtakes the subject's attempted summons.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: Pushing, collision, and prevention give the counter-summoned body its restraining function.

## vertical value reversal
- reading: Allah's sight reveals that apparent elevation can be distance and apparent abasement can be approach; the question silently tests the direction of the subject's movement.
- mechanism: Height and overflowing rise are set against the closing movement of lowering and approaching. A faint non-dominant extension image inside the obedience cue intensifies the contrast. Divine sight measures these vectors differently: self-elevation produces transgressive distance, while bodily descent produces nearness.
- trace:
  - 96:14 **ٱللَّهَ** ء ل ه B001: The worshipped one supplies the relational center relative to which elevation and approach are measured.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Divine sight observes the opposed bodily and moral directions rather than equating height with rank.
  - 96:1 **بِٱسْمِ** س م و B001: Elevation and height establish the vertical image at the opening.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Water rising into sweeping overflow makes transgression an uncontrolled upward excess.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B001: The non-dominant extension and rising image adds a remote vertical echo inside the rejected obedience relation.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B001: Lowering and humility reverse the transgressor's upward overflow through chosen descent.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Nearness supplies the paradoxical result of lowering: descent closes rather than increases distance.

## return limit and closing distance
- reading: The subject is visible across an arc already closing toward return and limit, so postponement cannot manufacture an outside to divine sight.
- mechanism: Sovereignty, return, terminal boundary, and approaching time form an accountability arc around the focus. Divine seeing is not a static camera within that arc; it is the impossibility of escaping by delay because conduct remains visible as return and limit draw nearer.
- trace:
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Continuous perception anchors the claim that temporal delay does not create an unseen interval.
  - 96:8 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Lordship and sovereignty identify the destination of return as an authoritative center.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B001: Returning to a prior point gives accountability a directed temporal movement.
  - 96:15 **يَنتَهِ** ن ه ي B002: The endpoint at which a thing terminates turns failure to desist into collision with a limit.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B002: Approaching time and a thing's completion make the final nearness temporal as well as spatial.

## protective gaze for refusal
- reading: Being seen has two live valences: it exposes coercion while assuring the worshipper that refusal, prostration, and approach occur under protective oversight.
- mechanism: Protection before the focus and the final refusal, prostration, and approach after it reverse the emotional valence of being seen. The same gaze that exposes the coercer can shelter the one who refuses coercive obedience and continues worship.
- trace:
  - 96:14 **ٱللَّهَ** ء ل ه B001: The worshipped one anchors the loyal relation within which being seen can be protective rather than merely threatening.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Divine perception supplies oversight shared by both the coercer and the protected worshipper.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: Repelling harm by a protective barrier opens the possibility that divine oversight safeguards right action.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B001: Submission and obedience identify the human demand that must be refused in favor of the higher relation.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B001: Humble lowering supplies the worshipper's enacted alternative to coerced compliance.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Nearness makes continued worship under observation a movement toward safety and relation.

## human image in the visual field
- reading: As a contained optical metaphor, the human becomes the image held within sight and cannot step outside the very field in which he appears.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B006: Visible appearance and mirror imagery provide the optical field in which a human image can be held.
  - 96:2 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B005: The human of the eye and reflected image makes created humanity an object already present within vision.
  - 96:5 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B005: The repeated human-image branch carries the optical metaphor into the teaching sequence.
  - 96:6 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B005: Its third occurrence places the transgressing human, not an abstract deed alone, inside the visual field.

## sight as reportable inscription
- reading: The act becomes legible, recordable, and reportable: divine sight initiates a chain in which the event cannot disappear when the moment passes.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B003: The non-dominant image of relaying reports turns what is seen into something capable of transmission.
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing mark makes the observed act identifiable enough to enter a report.
  - 96:1 **ٱقْرَأْ** ق ر ء B002: Reading and recitation supply the voiced retrieval side of a transmissible record.
  - 96:4 **عَلَّمَ** ع ل م B002: Teaching through a mark links imparted knowledge with externally recoverable signs.
  - 96:4 **بِٱلْقَلَمِ** ق ل م B003: The pared writing instrument supplies the material channel through which a seen mark can persist and be relayed.

## hydraulic containment
- reading: As a contained hydraulic analogy, the subject's overflow sits inside a larger reservoir and returning circulation; the gaze he ignores is also what exposes his unacknowledged need.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B005: A large gathered body of water makes knowledge a containing reservoir rather than an isolated datum.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: The non-dominant quenching image casts sight as a field that fills and satisfies rather than merely touches a surface.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Overflowing water supplies the uncontrolled self-expansion that mistakes excess for independence.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Self-sufficiency names the false claim that the overflowing vessel needs no source or container.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B006: Returning rain or water adds circulation and return to the hydraulic mechanism.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B008: Nearness associated with seeking water redirects need toward approach rather than self-enclosure.


# Focus 96:15

## voluntary to forced stop
- reading: The threat is an agency transfer: failure to stop oneself results in being stopped through forcible control of the body's leading point.
- mechanism: The actor is first left an internal route to cessation. If that voluntary brake fails, an outside force takes the body's forward control point and supplies restraint mechanically.
- trace:
  - 96:15 **يَنتَهِ** ن ه ي B001: Restraint from an act supplies the voluntary brake whose failure triggers the condition.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Seizure by the forelock supplies the coercive grip that replaces failed self-restraint.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The forelock as a graspable front-of-head point supplies the bodily handle on which imposed control acts.

## endpoint delivery
- reading: The condition asks whether he reaches his limit willingly; otherwise pursuit makes his body encounter that limit.
- mechanism: Failure to arrive at the proper limit does not leave the motion open-ended. Pursuit reaches the actor, catches the forelock, and physically delivers him to an imposed endpoint.
- trace:
  - 96:15 **يَنتَهِ** ن ه ي B002: The endpoint reached by a thing turns cessation into arrival at a limit.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B005: Pursuit supplies the motion by which an avoided limit catches up with the actor.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The seized forelock supplies the terminal capture point that ends the pursuit.

## failed inner brake
- reading: It also contrasts two control systems: restraining reason within and a grasp imposed on the head when reason fails.
- mechanism: Reason is an internal inhibitor. When that inhibitor does not govern conduct, the promised seizure externalizes its missing function as bodily restraint.
- trace:
  - 96:15 **يَنتَهِ** ن ه ي B003: Reason that restrains from wrong supplies the failed internal control system.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Forcible seizure supplies an external substitute for the reason that did not restrain.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The grasped front of the head localizes the replacement of cognitive control by physical control.

## visible forehead mark
- reading: A coexisting branch reading makes the action mark the offender at the forehead, converting concealed defiance into visible condition.
- mechanism: Alongside seizure, the action can leave a darkened or heat-altered sign on the visible forelock area. Continued conduct is thus threatened with involuntary visibility, not only displacement.
- trace:
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B002: Reddish blackening supplies a visible alteration at the threatened bodily site.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B003: Light scorching by fire or hot wind supplies a causal route for the color change.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The forelock supplies the conspicuous bodily surface on which the alteration becomes legible.

## headship capture
- reading: The literal grip remains primary, but it may simultaneously collapse the individual's claim to be the foremost or controlling social part.
- mechanism: The body's foremost tuft and a group's foremost rank overlap as images of headship. Seizing the forelock can therefore carry a contained metonymic reading of capturing claimed preeminence.
- trace:
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B003: The select or noble part of a group supplies a social-headship resonance within the focus noun.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Forelock seizure supplies the physical capture from which the headship metonymy extends.
  - 96:15 **يَنتَهِ** ن ه ي B001: Refusal to cease supplies the conduct that puts both bodily freedom and asserted standing under restraint.

## counter inscription
- reading: The context turns them into a forensic mark: conduct that resists knowledge is made publicly legible on the forelock.
- mechanism: Naming as visible sign, knowledge as a distinguishing mark, writing by a pen, and the later attribution of falsehood to the forelock make the focus color/scorch branches behave like compulsory counter-inscription. A self-presentation that misstates its condition is overwritten at the body's most public front.
- trace:
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B002: Dark coloration supplies the involuntary visible notation.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B003: Scorching supplies the material process that writes the notation onto appearance.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The exposed forelock supplies the inscription surface.
  - 96:1 **بِٱسْمِ** س م و B001: The non-dominant visible-mark branch makes naming participate in public identification.
  - 96:4 **عَلَّمَ** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing sign that guides recognition supplies the epistemic function of the mark.
  - 96:4 **بِٱلْقَلَمِ** ق ل م B003: The prepared writing instrument supplies the context's explicit inscription channel.
  - 96:16 **كَٰذِبَةٍ** ك ذ ب B001: Falsehood predicated immediately of the forelock supplies what the counter-mark exposes.

## enforced reattachment
- reading: It is also an enforced reattachment that exposes self-sufficiency as a temporary misreading of the human condition.
- mechanism: The human begins as something attached, then imagines himself independent, yet is assigned a return. The focus seizure reverses that fantasy by reattaching the self-styled independent actor to an authority and trajectory he cannot evade.
- trace:
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: The grasp supplies the enforced attachment that contradicts imagined independence.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The forelock supplies the bodily attachment point through which return is imposed.
  - 96:2 **عَلَقٍ** ع ل ق B001: Attachment and adherence supply the human's initial condition of dependence.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: Self-sufficiency supplies the denial of attachment that the focus action reverses.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B001: Return to a prior point supplies the larger trajectory completed by the bodily capture.

## hydraulic arrest
- reading: The wider sequence makes stopping a hydraulic event: unchecked excess is caught and settled at a limit imposed through the forelock.
- mechanism: Transgression is imaged as water rising beyond its bound; the focus cessation root can image the terminal basin where floodwater settles, and return can image returning water. The seizure becomes the bodily arrest point of an overflow that would not settle itself.
- trace:
  - 96:15 **يَنتَهِ** ن ه ي B004: A pool at a flood's endpoint supplies the receiving limit where uncontrolled flow finally settles.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Forelock seizure supplies the force that arrests and channels the human overflow.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The graspable front supplies a concrete capture point for the arrest.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B002: Rising, sweeping water supplies the context's material model of transgressive excess.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B006: Returning rain or water adds a counter-current to the earlier overflow.

## prohibition reversed
- reading: It reverses his own prohibition onto him: the attempted controller must release control or be forcibly deprived of it.
- mechanism: The same restraint root first describes someone forbidding another, then becomes the condition that he himself must cease. Commands toward protection and the final refusal of obedience adjudicate the contest: the would-be controller loses jurisdiction and is placed under control.
- trace:
  - 96:15 **يَنتَهِ** ن ه ي B001: Cessation in the focus reverses restraint back onto the person who had exercised it.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Seizure supplies the enforcement that follows refusal to relinquish unauthorized control.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: The earlier act of forbidding identifies the offender as an attempted restrainer of another.
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: Binding command supplies the competing directive against which the offender's prohibition is judged.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: Protection from harm supplies the proper end of the competing command.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B001: The prohibition on yielding obedience finally cancels the offender's claim to command.

## opposed head postures
- reading: It marks the coercive counterpart to prostration: one head is forced out of dominance, while another lowers willingly and approaches.
- mechanism: The servant's chosen worship culminates in voluntarily lowering the head and thereby drawing near. The aggressor's head is instead controlled from the forelock. The focus threat becomes one half of a bodily polarity between willing abasement that produces nearness and coerced lowering that removes dominance.
- trace:
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: The forcible forelock grip supplies involuntary control of the aggressor's head.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The front of the head supplies the bodily point contrasted with voluntary prostration.
  - 96:10 **عَبْدًا** ع ب د B003: Humble worship supplies the servant's freely chosen relation of submission.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B003: The specified worship act supplies the action the aggressor had tried to obstruct.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B004: Bowing the head supplies the voluntary downward posture that mirrors the threatened grip.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Drawing near supplies the opposite relational result of the servant's lowered posture.

## cognition externalized
- reading: It follows a collapse of self-government: taught and seen, he still lets neither knowledge nor restraining reason govern, so government arrives as a grip on the head.
- mechanism: Teaching and knowing establish possible understanding; self-regarding judgment then supports imagined sufficiency, while divine seeing closes the epistemic circuit immediately before the threat. When reason does not inhibit conduct despite knowledge and visibility, the focus grip externalizes the missing inhibition.
- trace:
  - 96:15 **يَنتَهِ** ن ه ي B003: Reason named for restraining wrong supplies the internal faculty that should have stopped the action.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: The physical grip supplies an external inhibitor after cognitive inhibition fails.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The front of the head localizes the displacement of mental government by bodily government.
  - 96:5 **عَلَّمَ يَعْلَمْ** ع ل م B001: Disclosure to a knower supplies the context's offered capacity for understanding.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B002: Reflective opinion supplies the self-appraisal that wrongly concludes independence.
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B001: Knowing immediately before the threat makes the failure cognitive as well as behavioral.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: Sight and insight supply the enclosing observation under which the conduct occurs.

## forelock as moral agent
- reading: The next ayah turns it into the bodily front of the offending agency, so the seizure reads as targeted apprehension of false and culpably misdirected control.
- mechanism: The immediate continuation repeats the forelock and grammatically loads it with falsehood and culpable error. That localization makes the focus grasp less arbitrary: the seized point is presented as the embodied front of deceptive, misdirected agency.
- trace:
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Seizure supplies apprehension of the bodily point to which agency is attributed.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The literal forelock supplies the metonymic site where the person's conduct is localized.
  - 96:16 **نَاصِيَةٍ** ن ص ي B001: Immediate repetition keeps the same bodily site active while its moral description unfolds.
  - 96:16 **كَٰذِبَةٍ** ك ذ ب B001: Opposition to truth supplies the first moral property assigned to the forelock.
  - 96:16 **خَاطِئَةٍ** خ ط ء B002: Culpable fault supplies the second moral property and makes the grasp judicially targeted.

## social head extracted
- reading: The grip targets the social head itself, exposing the assembly's inability to shield the person who claims its foremost position.
- mechanism: A call to the offender's assembly is answered by a counter-call to agents of pushing and prevention. Against that social contest, the focus branch for the select or foremost group-member lets the singular forelock compress a collective hierarchy into its head, which the grip extracts from its protective gathering.
- trace:
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B003: The select or leading part of a group supplies the social head represented by the focus noun.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Forelock seizure supplies the extraction and defeat of that social head.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The literal bodily forelock keeps the social-head reading anchored in a concrete grasp.
  - 96:17 **فَلْيَدْعُ** د ع و B001: Calling by speech supplies the offender's attempt to mobilize support.
  - 96:17 **نَادِيَهُۥ** ن د و B001: The non-dominant assembly branch supplies the collective body on which the offender relies.
  - 96:18 **سَنَدْعُ** د ع و B001: The answering call supplies a symmetrical but superior mobilization.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: Pushing, impact, and prevention supply the counter-assembly's operative force.

## deceptive presentation overwritten
- reading: As a contained material analogy, it replaces a groomed, deceptive presentation with an involuntary mark that tells the body's condition more truthfully.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B007: Wearing dyed garments supplies the chosen layer of outward presentation.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B002: Dark coloration supplies the involuntary replacement of that presentation.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B002: Groomed or lengthened hair supplies self-fashioned appearance at the threatened site.
  - 96:16 **كَٰذِبَةٍ** ك ذ ب B009: A garment that misstates its condition supplies the exact analogy of deceptive appearance.
  - 96:1 **بِٱسْمِ** س م و B001: A visible identifying mark supplies the public legibility of the overwritten appearance.

## vision as tether
- reading: In the split-root resonance, being fully seen already functions like a tether; the future forelock grip makes that prior inescapability bodily explicit.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Forelock seizure supplies the overt physical binding that manifests the latent tether.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The graspable forelock supplies the tether's bodily attachment point.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B007: The non-dominant binding-rope branch lets encompassing sight resonate as a tether already around the actor.
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B001: Known disclosure supplies the condition of inescapable legibility that makes the tether image functional.

## summons materializes as force
- reading: As a contained kinetic echo, the calls gather into pursuit and pushing until the verbal contest acquires a bodily point of impact at the forelock.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B005: Pursuit supplies convergent motion toward the forelock.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B004: Striking supplies the impact into which the rival summons materialize.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The forelock supplies the bodily convergence point of pursuit and impact.
  - 96:17 **فَلْيَدْعُ** د ع و B001: The ordinary call supplies the first verbal mobilization.
  - 96:18 **سَنَدْعُ** د ع و B001: The non-dominant severe-push branch gives the answering summons a contained kinetic echo.
  - 96:18 **ٱلزَّبَانِيَةَ** ز ب ن B001: Pushing, impact, and prevention stabilize the kinetic echo in the immediate context.

## neck axis
- reading: The split-root neck image activates a vertical axis on which proud extension is capturable, while chosen head-bowing becomes the route to nearness.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: Forelock seizure supplies forced control at the top of the neck-head axis.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The front of the head supplies the axis's upper bodily point.
  - 96:19 **تُطِعْهُ** ط و ع B002: The non-dominant long-neck branch activates vertical extension as a contrastive posture.
  - 96:19 **وَٱسْجُدْ** س ج د B004: Bowing the head supplies voluntary flexion of the same bodily axis.
  - 96:19 **وَٱقْتَرِب** ق ر ب B001: Drawing near supplies the relational result of voluntary rather than coerced lowering.


# Focus 96:16

## embodied control point
- reading: The agent's graspable control point is itself presented as the bodily source and bearer of false action and deliberate wrongdoing.
- mechanism: The forelock is both a body part and a graspable point of control. Grammatical agreement relocates false speech or action and deliberate wrongdoing from an unnamed owner onto that controlling front, making moral agency bodily and seizable.
- trace:
  - 96:16 **نَاصِيَةٍ** ن ص ي B001: The branch's forelock and seizure by it supplies both the concrete body part and its function as a graspable control point.
  - 96:16 **كَٰذِبَةٍ** ك ذ ب B001: The branch's falsehood against truth in speech or action makes the first adjective an active distortion rather than a decorative insult.
  - 96:16 **خَاطِئَةٍ** خ ط ء B002: The branch's culpable and intentional wrongdoing makes the second adjective mark accountable agency rather than accidental error.

## failed leading trajectory
- reading: A foremost agent is diagnosed as a failed lead: its claim does not hold in action and its course misses what is right.
- mechanism: The forelock's select or foremost branch supplies a leading edge; falsehood as a charge that fails supplies a test of enacted truth; error as missing the right direction supplies the failed course. Together they depict a leader or front that cannot make its claim true in motion and veers from what is right.
- trace:
  - 96:16 **نَاصِيَةٍ** ن ص ي B003: The branch's chosen elite, noble leaders, and forerunners supplies the foremost or leading element in the trajectory.
  - 96:16 **كَٰذِبَةٍ** ك ذ ب B004: The branch's charge proving false when it is not carried through turns truth into a test of whether the lead action fulfills its claim.
  - 96:16 **خَاطِئَةٍ** خ ط ء B001: The branch's missing or passing beyond the right direction supplies the trajectory's deviation.

## autonomy overruns boundary
- reading: It identifies the front of a false autonomy drive: self-sufficiency becomes boundary-crossing, directional error, and an eventual loss of self-direction.
- mechanism: The context turns the exploratory leading-trajectory baseline into a causal arc. A self-judgment of independence precedes rebellious limit-crossing, while return closes the arc; the focus forelock becomes the leading edge of an autonomy claim that overshoots and cannot remain self-directed.
- trace:
  - 96:16 **نَاصِيَةٍ** ن ص ي B001: The branch's forelock and figurative control by taking it anchors the autonomy arc in a bodily control point.
  - 96:16 **كَٰذِبَةٍ** ك ذ ب B001: The branch's falsehood in speech or action characterizes the autonomy claim as untrue in both assertion and conduct.
  - 96:16 **خَاطِئَةٍ** خ ط ء B001: The branch's passing beyond or missing what is right gives the focus error a directional endpoint.
  - 96:6 **لَيَطْغَىٰٓ** ط غ ي B001: The branch's exceeding a limit in rebellion supplies the boundary-crossing action that the focus forelock leads.
  - 96:7 **رَّءَاهُ** ر ء ي B002: The branch's inward opinion and reflection supplies the self-assessment that initiates the mechanism.
  - 96:7 **ٱسْتَغْنَىٰٓ** غ ن ي B001: The branch's wealth or self-sufficiency supplies the content of the deceptive autonomy judgment.
  - 96:8 **ٱلرُّجْعَىٰٓ** ر ج ع B001: The branch's return or restoration supplies the reversal that prevents the self-directed course from remaining final.

## counter command misdirects
- reading: They define a misdirecting authority: a false command head suppresses a rightly oriented and protective practice.
- mechanism: The focus defects become socially operative rather than private. Prohibition blocks worship that is aligned with guidance, while the opposed command directs toward protection; read through the focus's leadership branch, the forelock is a command head whose false judgment diverts others from the right course.
- trace:
  - 96:16 **نَاصِيَةٍ** ن ص ي B003: The branch's elite, noble, or leading stratum supplies the focus forelock's exploratory role as a command head.
  - 96:16 **كَٰذِبَةٍ** ك ذ ب B002: The branch's declaring a person or report false supplies the authority's rejecting judgment.
  - 96:16 **خَاطِئَةٍ** خ ط ء B001: The branch's deviation from the proper direction makes the command head's effect one of misrouting.
  - 96:9 **يَنْهَىٰ** ن ه ي B001: The branch's restraint and prohibition supplies the blocking act through which the focus defects affect another person.
  - 96:10 **صَلَّىٰٓ** ص ل و B003: The branch's specific act of worship identifies the practice being obstructed by the counter-command.
  - 96:11 **ٱلْهُدَىٰٓ** ه د ي B001: The branch's gentle direction toward the path and truth supplies the right course against which the focus error is measured.
  - 96:12 **أَمَرَ** ء م ر B002: The branch's request and obligation supplies the opposed, constructive command.
  - 96:12 **بِٱلتَّقْوَىٰٓ** و ق ي B001: The branch's warding off harm by protection supplies the constructive command's protective destination.

## denial turns but is seen
- reading: It condenses an exposed sequence: a false verdict is issued, the agent turns from it, and the resulting direction is visibly inscribed on the agent's front.
- mechanism: The context unfolds the focus adjectives as a temporal sequence. Active denial is paired with turning away; knowing and seeing then expose that movement. The focus compresses this into a bodily result-state: a front marked by the false verdict it issued and by the direction it chose.
- trace:
  - 96:16 **نَاصِيَةٍ** ن ص ي B001: The branch's frontal body part supplies the visible result-state onto which the preceding actions are compressed.
  - 96:16 **كَٰذِبَةٍ** ك ذ ب B001: The branch's falsehood against truth makes the focus adjective the settled quality produced by denial.
  - 96:16 **خَاطِئَةٍ** خ ط ء B001: The branch's deviation from the proper direction lets the focus adjective register the consequence of turning away.
  - 96:13 **كَذَّبَ** ك ذ ب B002: The branch's attribution of falsehood to a person or report supplies the active denial that precedes the focus adjective.
  - 96:13 **وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ** و ل ي B007: The branch's turning back and withdrawal supplies the physical and volitional direction of the error.
  - 96:14 **يَعْلَم** ع ل م B001: The branch's disclosure of a thing to a knower supplies the epistemic exposure that the denial cannot evade.
  - 96:14 **يَرَىٰ** ر ء ي B001: The branch's sight and insight supplies the observing perspective under which the false turn becomes legible.

## control point reversed
- reading: It is the control handle where false and culpable agency is reversed: the point that led is made the point by which its owner is led.
- mechanism: The preceding action resolves the body part as the precise handle of capture. The focus adjectives then do more than identify an offender: they explain why the point that projected agency and control is itself taken under control.
- trace:
  - 96:16 **نَاصِيَةٍ** ن ص ي B001: The branch's forelock, pulling, and figurative control supplies the focus object as an agency-bearing handle.
  - 96:16 **كَٰذِبَةٍ** ك ذ ب B001: The branch's false speech or action supplies the failed exercise of agency that motivates reversal.
  - 96:16 **خَاطِئَةٍ** خ ط ء B002: The branch's culpable intentional wrongdoing makes the reversal accountable rather than arbitrary.
  - 96:15 **لَنَسْفَعًۢا** س ف ع B001: The branch's taking by the forelock supplies the explicit capture action that activates the focus noun's control image.
  - 96:15 **بِٱلنَّاصِيَةِ** ن ص ي B001: The repeated branch's forelock and seizure image links the preceding definite object directly to the focus apposition.

## two modes of lowering
- reading: It is one pole of a final posture contrast: the lying command head is forcibly mastered, while refusal of that command and voluntary lowering produce nearness.
- mechanism: The close creates a contrastive body grammar. One control point is taken by force; the addressee refuses obedience to its owner, lowers the head voluntarily, and thereby draws near. The focus phrase marks the command source whose coerced lowering must not be confused with chosen devotional lowering.
- trace:
